We release our code on~\href{https://github.com/gerritq/mgt_probes}{github}.

</details>


### [129] [Structurally-bounded Agentic Graph Exploration for Evidence-Grounded Scholarly DeepSearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24809)
*Rima Hazra,Sayan Layek,Somnath Banerjee,Soumen Chakrabarti,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Crase 是一种有界、可检查的学术搜索方法，替代深度研究代理。它仅查询一次搜索引擎获取种子论文，沿 1.5 跳引用邻域扩展，剪除缺乏蕴含支持的引用边，并用考虑新颖度的随机游走对剩余论文排序。在 LitSearch 等基准上，Crase 以约三分之一的成本在 recall@50 上比基于专有模型的深度研究代理最高提升 3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究代理采用开放式搜索循环，候选集、保留理由和停止条件都不透明且不可预知，导致成本高、可检查性差；学术搜索需要一种有界、可检查且高效的替代方案。

Method: Crase 的流程是固定的：1) 对搜索引擎仅查询一次获取种子论文；2) 沿论文的 1.5 跳引用邻域扩展候选；3) 剪除引用边中缺乏蕴含（entailment）支持的边；4) 用考虑新颖度（recency-aware）的随机游走对剩余论文排序。候选集、论文保留理由及停止条件在推理前即显式固定。

Result: 在 LitSearch 及另一个基准上（500K 篇 arXiv 论文语料），Crase 在 recall@50 上比基于专有模型的深度研究代理最高提升 3 倍，且成本仅为后者的约三分之一。

Conclusion: 有界且可检查的搜索策略能够在学术检索任务上超越开放式深度研究代理，同时显著降低成本，说明透明、可控的推理管线是更优的设计选择。

Abstract: We present Crase, a bounded and inspectable alternative to deep research agents for scholarly search. Instead of an open-ended search loop, Crase queries a search engine once for seed papers, expands them along their 1.5-hop citation neighborhood, prunes citation edges whose claims lack entailment support, and ranks the remaining papers with a recency-aware random walk. This makes the candidate set, the reason each paper is kept, and the stopping condition explicit and fixed before inference. On LitSearch and one further benchmarks over a 500K-paper arXiv corpus, Crase outperforms deep research agents built on proprietary models by up to 3$\times$ recall@50 at roughly a third of the cost.

</details>


### [130] [Reading Is Not Using: Retrieval, Judgment, and the Design of AI Financial Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24842)
*Miao Liu,Zhizhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）在长上下文金融分析中存在"检索-整合鸿沟"：即使模型能准确检索到风险披露信息，当无关上下文从2,000增加到128,000个token时，该披露对投资判断的影响会降至实验噪声水平。该现象在多个模型家族、多种判断任务及真实10-K文件中稳定复现；更强模型只是推迟而非消除这一鸿沟。因果记忆干预实验表明，压缩摘要与源文本查找共同决定披露能否进入判断；工作流架构是决定性因素——分块-摘要流水线会驱逐相关信息，而紧邻决策点的目标化、结构化重述则能恢复披露的影响力。因此，AI分析师的表现由模型能力与工作流架构共同决定，基于检索的评测可能认证出那些忽略已检索信息做出判断的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM金融分析评测仅关注模型"能检索到什么"，而非"检索到的信息是否真正影响其判断"。这种评测导向忽略了检索与判断之间的整合环节，可能导致系统在检索准确的情况下依然做出不受该信息影响的投资决策，从而构成潜在的正确性隐患。

Method: 采用受控实验设计：固定目标公司信息，仅变化不相关上下文长度（2,000至128,000 token）；在多个模型家族、多种判断任务及真实10-K文件（移除真实披露）上进行验证；使用因果记忆干预（压缩摘要vs源文本查找）分解信息传递路径；比较两种工作流架构（分块-摘要流水线 vs 紧邻决策的目标化结构化重述）。

Result: 发现：1) 上下文变长后，风险披露对投资判断的影响降至噪声底限，即使直接检索依然准确；2) 该模式跨模型家族与判断任务复现，并在真实10-K文件上成立；3) 更强模型仅推迟鸿沟出现，并未消除；4) 压缩摘要与源文本查找共同承担信息传递；5) 分块-摘要流水线会驱逐相关信息，而目标化结构化重述能恢复披露影响力。

Conclusion: AI分析师的绩效由模型能力与工作流架构共同决定，而非仅仅取决于模型本身或检索准确率。检索型评测可能对"已检索但未整合进判断"的系统给出虚假认证，因此评测应当覆盖从检索到判断整合的完整链路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as AI analysts to process financial disclosures and support AI-assisted investment decisions. Yet such systems are usually evaluated by what they can retrieve, not whether retrieved information affects their judgments. We identify a retrieval-integration gap in long-context financial analysis. Holding focal-firm information fixed and varying only unrelated context from 2,000 to 128,000 tokens, we find that a risk disclosure's influence on investment judgments falls to the experimental noise floor even as direct retrieval remains accurate. The pattern replicates across model families and judgment tasks and in experiments removing real disclosures from actual 10-K filings. More capable models postpone but do not eliminate the gap. Causal memory interventions show that compressed summaries and source-text lookup jointly transmit disclosures into judgments. Workflow architecture determines whether this transmission succeeds: chunk-and-summarize pipelines evict relevant information, whereas a targeted, structured restatement adjacent to the decision restores its influence. AI analyst performance is therefore jointly determined by model capability and workflow architecture. Retrieval-based evaluations can certify systems whose investment judgments ignore information they demonstrably retrieved.

</details>


### [131] [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24848)
*Fei Tang,Huawen Shen,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengxi Lu,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BrowserForge 是一个通过在开放互联网上并行驱动大量浏览器沙箱、以规模化生成网页代理交互轨迹数据的框架。它结合开放网页数据源、沙箱集群管理和“提案者-求解者”双智能体循环三大组件，最终产出一个包含 203,238 条轨迹、每条来自不同网站的语料库（规模和多样性均超过以往数据集）。仅依赖截图的紧凑多模态模型在该语料上微调后，在在线 Online-Mind2Web 上的成功率从 25.66% 提升到 33.33%，并在静态 Multimodal-Mind2Web 上持续提升步骤准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于像素（截图）行动的网页代理可避免直接解析 HTML 或可访问性树的脆弱性与高昂 token 开销，但其训练高度依赖大规模高质量交互轨迹，而如何规模化生产这类数据仍是悬而未决的问题。现有公开数据集仅含几千条轨迹且来源于固定狭窄的网站集合，自动化合成管线也局限于预定义站点或教程来源，代理所见的独特网站数量几乎不增长，制约了代理的泛化能力。

Method: BrowserForge 由三个组件耦合而成：(1) 开放网页数据源阶段——让代理接触数十万个真实、公开可达的网站；(2) 沙箱集群管理器——以高利用率并行调度数百个并发浏览器；(3) “提案者-求解者”双智能体循环——将原始页面转化为可执行任务，并收集经过验证的交互轨迹。随后通过规则加模型的清洗管线移除失败运行，并将存留的推理重写为统一风格的思维链。页面结构（如可访问性树）仅作为合成阶段的信号用于标注/验证，而训练与发布出的代理完全只依据截图行动。

Result: 生成的语料包含 203,238 条轨迹，每条均来自不同网站，规模和多样性优于此前的轨迹数据集。在紧凑多模态模型上微调后：在线上 Online-Mind2Web 基准上的成功率从 25.66% 提升至 33.33%；在静态 Multimodal-Mind2Web 上步骤准确率持续提升，且增益随语料规模扩大而增长。受控分析进一步证实开放网页数据源与广泛的网站覆盖是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该工作证明：以开放互联网为数据来源、借助并行沙箱与双智能体循环可规模化生产高质量、高多样性的网页交互轨迹，从而有效提升纯截图驱动的网页代理能力。开放网页数据源和广泛的网站覆盖是成功的关键，为网页代理训练数据的规模化提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Web agents that act from rendered pixels avoid the fragility and heavy token cost of reading a page's HTML or accessibility tree, but training them depends on large amounts of high-quality interaction trajectories, and how to produce such data at scale remains an open problem. Public datasets typically contain only a few thousand trajectories drawn from a fixed and narrow set of websites, and even recent automated synthesis pipelines stay bound to predefined site lists or tutorial sources, so the number of distinct websites the agent ever sees barely grows. We present BrowserForge, a framework that generates web interaction data at scale by driving many browser sandboxes in parallel over the open web. BrowserForge couples three components: an open-web sourcing stage that exposes the agent to hundreds of thousands of real, openly reachable websites; a sandbox cluster manager that schedules hundreds of concurrent browsers with high utilization; and a Proposer-Solver dual-agent loop that turns a raw page into an executable task and then collects a verified trajectory for it. A rule-plus-model cleaning pipeline removes failed runs and rewrites the surviving reasoning into a single unified chain-of-thought style. Page structure such as the accessibility tree is used only as a synthesis-time signal; the agent we train and release acts purely from the screenshot. The resulting corpus contains 203,238 trajectories, each collected from a distinct website, larger and more diverse than prior trajectory datasets. Fine-tuning a compact multimodal model on this corpus raises its success rate on the live Online-Mind2Web from 25.66% to 33.33% and consistently improves step accuracy on the static Multimodal-Mind2Web, with the gain growing as the corpus scales. Controlled analyses further confirm that open-web sourcing and broad website coverage are key contributors to the observed improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Task-disentangled Low-Rank Adaptation for Versatile Audio-visual Multi-modal Learning Tasks within a Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24209)
*Hanyu Xuan,Mengqi Zhang,Junjun Mao,Fei Wang,Kun Li,Guanghui Yue,Zhiliang Wu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向多项视听多模态学习(AVMML)任务的统一框架，通过基于大语言模型的任务解耦LoRA机制，实现任务特有知识与任务共享知识的动态整合，从而在多任务联合训练中减少相互干扰，超越现有统一模型及多数任务特定模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVMML研究大多孤立地处理每个任务，与人类统一的多模态认知能力相悖；但简单的多任务联合训练会因任务间复杂关系而产生相互干扰，因此需要一种能同时容纳多种AVMML任务并缓解干扰的统一框架。

Method: 借助大语言模型强大的表示与泛化能力，设计任务解耦的低秩适配(LoRA)机制，包含三个组件：任务通用低秩矩阵(捕获通用视听知识)、任务特定调制矩阵(解耦任务特有模式)、跨任务协作专家(利用任务间内在相关性)。通过模型视角与任务视角的双重显式协作实现多任务统一。

Result: 在多项AVMML任务上优于现有统一视听模型，并在部分任务上超越大多数任务特定模型。

Conclusion: 从模型与任务两个层面进行显式协作，可有效实现任务特有与共享知识解耦，从而在统一框架中获得高效的多任务协同能力。

Abstract: Inspired by human multi-modal perception, Audio-Visual Multi-Modal Learning (AVMML) integrates auditory and visual information to leverage complementary cross-modal cues, enabling more robust and comprehensive scene perception. Existing studies predominantly tackle each AVMML task in isolation, which stands in stark contrast to humans' unified cognitive capacity for handling versatile perception. However, naive joint training across multiple AVMML tasks often suffers from mutual interference, arising from the intricate inter-task relationships. To address this, we propose a unified framework that simultaneously accommodates versatile AVMML tasks. Specifically, benefiting from powerful representation and generalization capabilities of large language models, we design a task-disentangled Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) mechanism that enables dynamic integration of both task-specific and task-shared knowledge, thereby facilitating effective multi-task collaboration. The proposed task-disentangled LoRA comprises three components: a task-general low-rank matrix, task-specific modulation matrices, and cross-task collaboration experts, which respectively capture universal audio-visual knowledge, decouple task-specific pattern, and exploit inherent inter-task correlations. By unifying explicit collaboration from both model and task perspectives, our approach not only surpasses existing unified audio-visual models across multiple AVMML tasks, but also outperforms most task-specific models on certain AVMML tasks.

</details>


### [133] [EVEREST:Endogenous Vision-Language Reinforcement Reasoning Exploration for Urban Socio-Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24640)
*Qixiu Li,Zhongzhi He,Xiang Zhu,Xiaoyong Li,Jiarun Lin,Weifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出EVEREST模型，通过主动式第一人称探索策略和自我纠错机制解决城市社会-语义分割中的边界不精确问题，并以伪代码形式的自然语言提示规范执行逻辑，借助强化学习激发结构化推理，在真实城市社会-语义数据集上取得全部指标的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市社会-语义分割方法虽具备较高精度，但在目标边界刻画上仍不准确。根本原因在于当前模型主要依赖被动聚合的全局跨模态线索，缺乏对环境的主动探索与边界信息的针对性挖掘。

Method: 提出EVEREST模型：(1) 采用以自我为中心（egocentric）的探索策略，使模型主动探查边界线索并进行自我修正；(2) 将离散自然语言提示构建为伪代码，以规范模型的执行逻辑；(3) 引入强化学习实现该不可约的推理过程，激发模型的结构化推理能力。

Result: 在真实世界城市社会-语义数据集上，EVEREST在所有评价指标上均达到最优性能，验证了模型的有效性与优越性。

Conclusion: 主动探索与自我纠错机制结合结构化伪代码推理，可有效改善分割边界精度，为城市资源分配等下游应用提供更可靠的语义信息支持；代码已公开。

Abstract: Urban socio-semantic segmentation leverages digital and satellite imagery to provide critical spatial semantic information for downstream applications such as urban resource allocation. Although existing methods achieve high segmentation accuracy, they still suffer from inaccurate delineation of target boundaries. The underlying issue is that current models primarily rely on passively aggregated global cross-modal cues, lacking active exploration of the environment. To address this limitation, we propose the EVEREST model, which adopts an egocentric exploration strategy that enables the model to actively investigate boundary cues and perform self-correction. In addition, we formulate discrete natural-language prompts as pseudocode to regularize the execution logic. Reinforcement learning is further employed to implement this irreducible process and elicit the model's structured reasoning capability. Our EVEREST achieves optimal performance on all metrics in the real world urban socio-semantic dataset, demonstrating the superiority of our model. Codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EVEREST-9D21/.

</details>


### [134] [Learning to Prefer Reliably: Error-Augmented Emotion Preference Optimization with Calibrated Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24730)
*Zilong Huang,Junyi Peng,Junjie Li,Kai Li,Wenze Ren,Kong Aik Lee,Man-Wai Mak,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出EAPO（Error-Augmented Preference Optimization）框架，针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）情感偏好判断的两大缺陷——数据层面监督稀疏（每个正例仅有一个负例，缺乏语义流畅但情感不一致的描述）和模型层面单一MLLM裁判存在模型特有偏差——通过构建错误增强数据集（为每个偏好描述生成多个受控的、情感感知的负例）并采用多个独立MLLM裁判的边际校准软融合（margin-calibrated soft fusion）来改善情感偏好学习的可靠性。在MER2026-EmoPrefer挑战赛数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感偏好学习依赖成对比较来对齐MLLM与人类对开放式情感描述的判断，但存在两层局限：(1) 数据层面，传统成对监督稀疏，通常每个正例仅对应一个负例，无法覆盖情感描述出错的各种方式，尤其缺乏语义流畅但情感不一致的负样本；(2) 模型层面，依赖单一MLLM裁判，其判断在解读细粒度或模糊的多模态情感线索时易受模型特定偏差影响。

Method: EAPO框架在数据和模型两个层面同时改进：(1) 数据层面，从每个偏好描述出发生成多个受控且情感感知的负例描述，构建错误增强数据集，扩充监督信号覆盖面；(2) 模型层面，将多个独立的MLLM裁判适配到更丰富的监督中，并采用边际校准软融合（margin-calibrated soft fusion）将异构的偏好边际映射到统一尺度后再聚合，以缓解单一裁判偏差。官方代码已开源。

Result: 在MER2026-EmoPrefer挑战赛数据集和所构建的错误增强数据集上的实验表明，EAPO提升了情感偏好预测性能，并增强了MLLM裁判在评估“语义流畅但与视频多模态情感证据冲突的描述”时的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EAPO通过错误增强监督与多裁判软融合相结合，有效缓解了情感偏好学习中的数据稀疏与单裁判偏差问题，为提升MLLM开放式情感描述判断的可靠性和鲁棒性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Emotion preference learning uses pairwise comparisons between candidate descriptions to align multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with human judgments of open-ended emotion descriptions and to train reward models that capture human emotional preferences. However, conventional pairwise supervision is often sparse, typically providing only a single negative description for each positive description, and therefore offers limited coverage of the diverse ways in which an emotion description can be incorrect. In particular, models may be insufficiently exposed to semantically fluent but emotionally inconsistent descriptions. Beyond this data-level limitation, relying on a single MLLM judge introduces a distinct model-level concern: its judgments can be affected by model-specific biases when interpreting fine-grained or ambiguous multimodal emotional cues. To address these limitations, we propose Error-Augmented Preference Optimization (EAPO), a framework for improving the reliability of MLLM-based emotion preference judgment at both the data and model levels. First, we construct an error-augmented dataset by generating multiple controlled and emotion-aware negative descriptions from each preferred description. We then adapt multiple independent MLLM judges to this richer supervision and aggregate their preference margins using margin-calibrated soft fusion, which maps heterogeneous margins to a common scale before aggregation. Experiments on the MER2026-EmoPrefer Challenge dataset and our error-augmented dataset demonstrate that EAPO improves emotion preference prediction and enhances the robustness of MLLM judges when evaluating fluent descriptions that conflict with the video's multimodal emotional evidence. Our code is available at https://github.com/slash1028/EAPO-EmoPrefer.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Equivariant Cellular Sheaves for Molecular Electronic Structure: Bridging Sheaf Cohomology and E(3)-Equivariant Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23571)
*Krishna Harish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等变细胞层网络（Equivariant Cellular Sheaf Networks），将分子单粒子哈密顿量在对角化平移后视为分子规则胞腔复形上的细胞层拉普拉斯算子，从而用层论统一了等变消息传递网络与拓扑深度学习，并给出非键轨道计数、环电流/离域信息等拓扑不变量以及严格的可表达性与上同调对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变消息传递网络是分子性质与原子间势预测的标准模型，而拓扑深度学习的细胞层结构尚未与分子电子结构建立系统联系。作者的核心观察是：在局域原子轨道基下，经过常数平移获得半正定性的分子单粒子哈密顿量，正是由分子构造的规则胞腔复形上的细胞层拉普拉斯算子，由此可将 Slater-Koster 形式作为特例纳入、并带来可证明的拓扑不变性与表达能力。

Method: 将胞腔复形上的限制映射构造为 O(3)-可转向的双中心核函数（由键几何决定），得到 E(3) 与置换等变的算子；其特殊情形即 Slater-Koster 形式。利用第零层上同调 H^0=ker L 刻画非键（零模式）轨道，利用 Hodge 1-拉普拉斯的 H^1 让高维胞腔（环）携带环电流与离域信息；并证明了等变性、表达能力、反过平滑性质与上同调对应结果。

Result: 数值验证显示：哈密顿量到层的嵌入可达机器精度，上同调维数在十一个共轭分子上复现非键轨道计数，层拉普拉斯算子的 O(3) 等变性达机器精度；在有方向的电子结构目标上，该等变模型取得更低误差并具备旋转泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于对等变哈密顿量建模的层论形式化及其不变量，而非等变哈密顿量预测本身；模型严格推广了 E(3) 等变消息传递网络与 CW 网络，并继承非平凡层扩散的抗过平滑性质。

Abstract: Equivariant message-passing networks are the standard model for molecular property and interatomic-potential prediction, and recent work predicts the electronic Hamiltonian itself in an E(3)-equivariant way. Separately, topological deep learning has extended graph networks to cellular sheaves. Our central observation is structural: in a localized atomic-orbital basis, the molecular single-particle Hamiltonian, after a constant shift that makes it positive semidefinite, is the Laplacian of a cellular sheaf on a regular cell complex built from the molecule. Making the restriction maps O(3)-steerable two-center kernels from bond geometry recovers the Slater-Koster form as a special case and yields an E(3)- and permutation-equivariant operator. Three consequences follow. First, the zeroth sheaf cohomology H^0 = ker L is a topological invariant equal to the non-bonding (zero-mode) orbitals, recovering the classical alternant non-bonding-orbital count as a lower bound. Second, the Hodge 1-Laplacian lets higher cells (rings) carry cycle and delocalization information through H^1. Third, the model strictly generalizes E(3)-equivariant message-passing networks and CW networks, and inherits the anti-oversmoothing of non-trivial sheaf diffusion. We prove equivariance, expressivity, and cohomological-correspondence results for the Equivariant Cellular Sheaf Networks, and validate them numerically: the Hamiltonian-to-sheaf embedding is exact to machine precision, the cohomology dimension reproduces non-bonding-orbital counts across eleven conjugated molecules, the sheaf Laplacian is O(3)-equivariant to machine precision, and the equivariant model attains lower error and rotation generalization on a directional electronic target. Our contribution is this sheaf-theoretic formalization and its invariants, not equivariant Hamiltonian prediction itself.

</details>


### [136] [Data Predictability Shapes Weibull Weight-Scale Growth in Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23573)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer权重幅值服从两参数Weibull分布（形状k≈1.2在各层与模型间稳定，尺度λ承载训练引起的权重变化），并建立了一个训练前可计算的统计量——二元组条件熵D=H(下一个|前一个)——与λ增长之间的经验律：在多种受控损坏族下，λ²−λ₀²=C₀(η)+C₁(η)(H_r−D)^0.59，其中指数0.59来自独立测量的数据侧饱和关系而非直接拟合。移除每个学习率η的两个系数后，跨越一个数量级学习率的23次运行坍缩到同一幂律（斜率1，R²=0.941）。因D在训练前即可计算，该律是前向预测器：端到端自验证对留出样本内权重增长的相对误差仅5.7%，且在模型级/逐层分辨率及两种架构上均成立。其边界也被指出：跨语料预测对代码过预测，暗示冗余度是更广泛的数据到权重框架Φ(D,R,A,H)的第二个轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是理解训练如何将语料性质印刻到Transformer的权重上。已有工作表明权重可被Weibull分布刻画，但缺少决定其尺度λ增长的数据侧因素；作者希望找到“语料的什么属性决定了λ增长多少”，并得到一个训练前即可计算的、可前向预测权重增长的定律。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 用两参数Weibull分布（形状k≈1.2固定、尺度λ可变）刻画权重幅值，使λ承载训练引起的变化；(2) 引入训练前可计算的二元组条件熵D=H(next|prev)及匹配预算的洗牌基线H_r；(3) 在受控损坏（corruption）族上拟合出学习率条件化的定律λ²−λ₀²=C₀(η)+C₁(η)(H_r−D)^0.59，其中凸指数0.59并非对增长曲线直接拟合，而是继承自独立测量的数据侧饱和关系；(4) 从各运行中去除两个每η系数，检验23次运行（学习率跨度一个数量级）的坍缩一致性；(5) 进行端到端自验证（前向预测）与跨语料预测，并检查模型级、逐层分辨率及两种架构下的稳健性。

Result: 主要结果：去除每η的两个系数后，23次运行坍缩到(H_r−D)^0.59，斜率为1，R²=0.941（直接按每个η拟合则较弱，R²≈0.82）；端到端自验证预测留出语料内权重增长，相对误差5.7%；该读出现在模型级与逐层分辨率，并在两种架构上保持函数形式不变、仅系数改变。边界方面：跨语料预测对代码语料过预测，说明冗余度是第二个轴。

Conclusion: 结论是：Transformer权重增长主要被语料的二阶（二元组）可压缩性（以H_r−D衡量）所决定，并受学习率调制，形成一个训练前可计算的前向预测律；该律具有跨层、跨架构的稳定性，但其适用边界限定在受控族内，跨语料时需引入冗余度R等第二轴，指向更广泛的数据到权重框架Φ(D,R,A,H)。

Abstract: A trained transformer's weight magnitudes can be summarized by a two-parameter Weibull distribution whose shape $k \approx 1.2$ is stable across layers and models, so the scale $λ$ carries most training-induced movement. What corpus property sets how much $λ$ grows? Using the bigram conditional entropy $D = H(\text{next} \mid \text{prev})$, a training-free statistic computed before training, we find across controlled corruption families a learning-rate-conditioned law, $λ^2 - λ_0^2 = C_0(η) + C_1(η)(H_r - D)^{0.59}$, where $H_r$ is a matched-budget shuffle baseline. The convex exponent is inherited from an independently measured data-side saturation relation rather than fitted directly to the growth curve. After removing the two per-$η$ coefficients, 23 runs spanning an order of magnitude in learning rate collapse onto $(H_r - D)^{0.59}$ with unit slope ($R^2 = 0.941$; direct per-$η$ fits are weaker, $R^2 \approx 0.82$). Because $D$ is computed before training, the law is a forward predictor: an end-to-end self-validation recovers held-out within-family weight growth with 5.7% relative error. The readout holds at model and per-layer resolutions and across two tested architectures, with the functional form preserved and only the coefficients changing. It also marks its boundary: cross-corpus prediction over-predicts code, implicating redundancy as a second axis of a broader $Φ(D,R,A,H)$ data-to-weight framework.

</details>


### [137] [From Causal Plausibility to Causal Reliability: Evaluating LLMs as Calibrated Direct Causal-Edge Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23660)
*Amit Kumar,Elnur Adl Zarabi,Suranjana Trivedy,Zhiqian Chen,Lei Zhang,Kaiqun Fu,Taoran Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了12个指令微调开源大语言模型在六类基准因果图、五种提示策略和四种置信度来源下的直接因果边判断能力。研究发现：(1) 模型的因果判断显著偏向高召回，预测出过密且含大量假阳性边的图；(2) 模型常能捕捉因果关联，但难以可靠识别因果方向性与直接性，且对结构性错误预测高度过度自信（自报置信度≥80%的假阳性达80.8%/84.6%）；(3) 基于logit的置信度不可靠，而跨提示/跨模型一致性是更有前景的置信度信号，但优势在Holm校正后不显著。此外发现五个模型-数据集组合可能存在基准熟悉度问题。作者认为LLM更适合作为外部验证的软因果先验来源，而非因果结构的直接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益被用于为结构因果发现提供先验因果知识，但其直接边判断的可靠性和置信度是否可信尚未明确。因此需要一个系统、严格的评估来检验LLM在语言限定协议下提供的因果判断是否准确、校准好且可被信任。

Method: 对12个指令微调的开源权重模型，在6个基准因果图、5种提示策略以及4种置信度来源（口头表述、基于logit、跨提示一致性、跨模型一致性）下执行仅语言的成对因果判断协议。评估聚焦于召回/精度权衡、直接与方向性分类错误，并进行Holm校正后的置信度校准/判别分析，以及基准熟悉度审计（发现5个涉及AsiaM的模型-数据集对存在潜在熟悉度）。

Result: 三方面主要发现：(i) 判定呈强烈召回主导，生成过密图（大量假阳性边），提示仅移动精度-召回权衡而非解决过度预测；规模增益在最大图上减弱，且无法消除误校准。(ii) 模型常捕捉因果相关性，但无法可靠识别直接性或方向性——40.0%的间接边和36.0%的反向非边被误判为直接边（其他非边为28.2%）；其中80.8%/84.6%的假阳性获得≥80%的口头置信度，显示显著过度自信。(iii) 基于logit的置信度常坍缩在1.0附近，与正确性无关，而跨提示/跨模型一致性取得了更好的平均校准与判别能力，但Holm校正后差异不显著。

Conclusion: LLM不应被视为因果结构的直接证据来源，而更适合作为需外部验证的软因果先验来源；其置信度信号（尤其口头与logit型）不可靠，一致性指标相对更优但仍需谨慎。这为LLM辅助结构因果发现的实践提供了使用边界与信任校准的重要警示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide prior causal knowledge for structural causal discovery, yet whether their direct-edge judgments and confidence can be trusted remains unclear. We systematically evaluate 12 instruction-tuned open-weight models across six benchmark causal graphs, five prompting strategies, and four confidence sources: verbalized, logit-based, cross-prompt agreement, and cross-model agreement. Under our language-only pairwise protocol, our evaluation yields three key findings. (i) LLM-based causal judgments are strongly recall-dominant: models predict overly dense graphs with many false-positive edges, while prompting mainly shifts the precision-recall trade-off rather than resolving overprediction. Gains from model scale diminish on the largest graphs and do not eliminate miscalibration. (ii) LLMs often capture causal relatedness without reliably identifying directness or orientation. Relative to published reference graphs, models misclassify 40.0% of indirect and 36.0% of reversed non-edges as direct edges, versus 28.2% of other non-edges. Moreover, 80.8% and 84.6% of these false positives receive verbalized confidence of at least 80%, revealing substantial overconfidence in structurally incorrect predictions. (iii) Conventional confidence estimates are unreliable, whereas agreement offers a more promising signal. Logit-based confidence frequently collapses near 1.0 regardless of correctness, while cross-prompt and cross-model agreement achieve better mean calibration and discrimination, though their advantages are not statistically significant after Holm correction. A benchmark-familiarity audit further identifies potential familiarity in five model-dataset pairs, all involving AsiaM. Overall, our results suggest LLMs are better viewed as sources of externally validated soft causal priors than as direct evidence of causal structure.

</details>


### [138] [Renormalization Group Flow Matching for Scalable Local Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23696)
*Kanta Masuki,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出重正化群流匹配（RGFM），一种利用精确重正化群（RG）流作为概率路径的生成框架，将数据生成按空间尺度系统性地组织，从长波到短波结构逐级生成。借助RG的准局域性与尺度分离性质，RGFM可以用局部速度场近似概率流，生成范围仅为O(Λ^{-1}[\ln L+\ln(1/ε)])，从而以近线性的计算成本实现全局长程关联的建模。数值实验表明局部RGFM在1D分布和FFHQ图像上均显著优于局部流匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型面临根本性权衡：全局方法能保持完整的结构相干性但计算成本高，局部模型虽高效却难以复现长程关联和全局相干性。作者希望利用重正化群来弥合这一鸿沟——RG在不同空间尺度间无缝连接结构，在每一步保留准局域描述的同时保持长程关联，从而以局部计算实现全局结构生成。

Method: RGFM以精确RG流作为概率路径，按波长从长到短渐进生成数据。其核心在于利用RG的两个性质：准局域性与尺度分离。作者严格证明了RGFM概率流可由作用在空间范围O(Λ^{-1}[\ln L+\ln(1/ε)])上的局部速度场精确近似（Λ为RG波数尺度，L为线性系统尺寸，ε为容差），从而可用O(ln L)大小的patch进行局部生成建模，计算成本随系统体积近似线性增长。

Result: 数值实验表明，在一维代表性分布中，局部RGFM复现了远超其感受野的长程关联，而传统局部流匹配在长距离上存在显著误差；在FFHQ图像上，RGFM在64x64和256x256分辨率下生成比局部流匹配更为连贯、质量更高的样本。

Conclusion: RG引导的概率流为可扩展生成建模提供了一条有希望的新路径：仅使用局部计算即可捕捉长程结构。这打破了全局相干性与计算效率之间的传统权衡，为高分辨率、大尺度系统的生成建模奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite their remarkable success in modeling complex data, generative models face a fundamental tradeoff. Global approaches can capture full structural coherence but suffer from high computational costs, while local models are efficient but often fail to reproduce long-range correlations and global coherence. The renormalization group (RG) bridges this gap by seamlessly connecting spatial structures across different length scales, retaining quasi-local descriptions at each step while preserving long-range correlations. We introduce renormalization group flow matching (RGFM), a generative framework that systematically structures data generation across different spatial scales. By using an exact RG flow as the probability path, RGFM progressively generates data from long- to short-wavelength structures. To reconcile scalability with global structure, we exploit two key properties of the RG: quasi-locality and scale separation. We rigorously show that the RGFM probability flow can be accurately approximated by local velocity fields acting over a spatial range $O(Λ^{-1}[\ln L+\ln(1/\varepsilon)])$ for RG wavenumber scale $Λ$, linear system size $L$, and prescribed error tolerance $\varepsilon$. This property enables local generative modeling with patches of size $O(\ln L)$ and a computational cost that scales nearly linearly with the system volume. We numerically demonstrate that local RGFM reproduces long-range correlations far beyond its receptive field in representative one-dimensional distributions, while conventional local flow matching exhibits substantial errors at long distances. On FFHQ images, RGFM yields far more coherent and higher-quality samples than local flow matching at 64x64 and 256x256. Our results establish RG-guided probability flows as a promising route toward scalable generative modeling that captures long-range structure using only local computation.

</details>


### [139] [Response Renormalization for Critical Deep Equilibrium Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23725)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出响应重整化（Response Renormalization），一种用于深度平衡模型（DEQ）反向传播的框架，通过在低维临界子空间中对近奇异的残差雅可比本征模进行选择性校正（CMR与Phi-CMR），抑制伴随系统的梯度放大，同时保留良态敏感通道的梯度信息。在23个多物理场族、跨静态与瞬态任务的实验中，该方法在98%以上的静态和95%以上的瞬态比较中，测试误差较精确隐式微分训练模型高出不超过5%，证明其能提升优化可靠性而不损失有效学习信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEQ通过隐式微分训练，需求解由残差雅可比构成的伴随系统。当该雅可比沿损失敏感方向近乎奇异时，微小扰动会在伴随响应中被强烈放大，产生大且高度敏感的梯度，导致优化不可靠。现有方法缺乏对这类临界伴随放大的针对性控制。

Method: 提出响应重整化反向传播框架：仅提升（lift）选定的近极点分母，而不改变未提升的响应通道。(1) CMR在低维临界子空间中应用该校正，并给出稠密与矩阵自由两种集体化公式；(2) Phi-adaptive CMR基于正磁化率规则计算有界响应质量；(3) 区分修正冻结锚残差的精确梯度与反向响应替代（surrogate）；(4) 扩展至结构化隐式层与矢量吸引子（SILVA）。

Result: 在涵盖偏微分方程、三维场、算子映射、复杂几何与粒子系统的23个多物理场族上，CMR与Phi-CMR在超过98%的静态和95%的瞬态族-种子比较中，测试误差不超过精确隐式微分模型的5%。求解器索引实验显示其收敛于静态伴随解，物理时间滚动预测在评测条件下保持保真度。

Conclusion: 选择性响应重整化能在不全局抑制良态敏感性的前提下控制近临界伴随放大，使参数更新更可靠，同时保留学习所需的有效梯度信息，是精确隐式微分的一种实用且高保真的替代方案。

Abstract: Deep Equilibrium Models (DEQs) compute predictions from a hidden representation unchanged by the model update. Training through this equilibrium uses implicit differentiation and requires solving an adjoint system built from the residual Jacobian. If this Jacobian is nearly singular along loss-sensitive directions, small perturbations can be strongly amplified in the adjoint response, producing large, highly sensitive gradients that can make optimization unreliable. We introduce Response Renormalization, a backward-pass framework that lifts selected near-pole denominators while leaving unlifted response channels unchanged. Collective Mode Response Renormalization (CMR) applies this correction in a low-dimensional critical subspace, while Phi-adaptive CMR computes a bounded response mass from a positive susceptibility rule. We derive dense and matrix-free collective formulations, distinguish exact gradients of a modified frozen-anchor residual from backward-response surrogates, and extend the construction to Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors (SILVA). Across 23 multiphysics families spanning partial differential equations, three-dimensional fields, operator maps, complex geometries, and particle systems, CMR and Phi-CMR yield test errors no more than five percent higher than those from models trained with exact implicit differentiation in more than 98% of static and 95% of transient family-seed comparisons. Solver-index experiments show convergence toward the static adjoint, while physical-time rollouts retain predictive fidelity under the evaluated conditions. These results demonstrate that selective response renormalization can control near-critical adjoint amplification without globally damping well-conditioned sensitivity. Therefore, the method can make parameter updates more reliable while preserving the useful gradient information needed for learning.

</details>


### [140] [Calibration-Preserving Pruning: Compression as a Reliability Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23744)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Adria Binte Habib,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“校准保持剪枝”（Calibration-Preserving Pruning, CPP），在剪枝评分中加入非一致性梯度显著性，并采用剪枝、验证选择、一致性校准、测试四段分离的数据划分，从而在保证 split conformal 有限样本边际覆盖的前提下，提升剪枝后模型的预测集效率（更小的有效预测集）。实验表明在 50% 稀疏度下，CPP 在大标签分类任务上收益最大，但匹配对照组显示通用监督梯度贡献了大部分收益，且真实标签 CPP 与 Wanda+SNIP 无统计显著差异；阈值感知的候选标签 CPP 能以显式计算成本取得更优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，一旦剪枝模型在独立于一致性校准划分的条件下固定下来，split conformal prediction 就能提供有限样本边际覆盖保证，该保证并不依赖具体剪枝规则。因此核心问题从“覆盖率”转向“效率”：剪枝是否能在足够保留评分几何结构的前提下，得到更小的有效预测集。论文旨在回答这一效率问题，并设计能同时兼顾覆盖保证与集合缩减的剪枝方法。

Method: CPP 方法在基础剪枝评分（如 SparseGPT、Wanda）之上叠加非一致性梯度显著性（nonconformity-gradient saliency），并强制采用互不相交的四个划分：剪枝划分、验证选择划分、一致性校准划分和测试划分。理论上，有界评分扰动可推出有界的一致性分位数偏移和受控的集合膨胀，从而维持覆盖定理的有效性。配套实验使用 5 次独立种子的 Qwen2.5-1.5B，在 50% 稀疏度、15 个数据集-稀疏度单元上比较 CPP-SparseGPT、CPP-Wanda 与匹配对照组（Wanda+SNIP、真实标签/候选标签变体），并辅以 RoBERTa-base 和 Llama-3-8B 作为迁移诊断。

Result: 在 DBpedia-14 上，CPP-SparseGPT 将平均集合大小从 10.1 降至 8.6，同时准确率从 0.347 升至 0.366；CPP-Wanda 将集合大小从 11.2 降至 9.0，但准确率从 0.310 降至 0.295（存在权衡）。在 15 个数据集-稀疏度单元中，CPP-SparseGPT 在 13 个单元上获得更小集合、在 11 个单元上获得更高准确率。匹配对照显示通用监督梯度解释了大部分增益：真实标签的 CPP 与匹配的 Wanda+SNIP 无统计显著差异；而阈值感知的候选标签 CPP 在显式准确率与离线计算成本下达到 7.8 的平均集合大小。RoBERTa-base 与 Llama-3-8B 的诊断结果支持方法可迁移。

Conclusion: CPP 表明在固定剪枝模型后，覆盖保证由 split conformal 给出，剪枝规则的作用在于效率；通过梯度显著性增强的剪枝能够在保持有效预测集覆盖的同时缩减集合大小，尤其在大标签任务上。但消融与匹配对照提示，收益主要来自通用监督梯度而非真实标签信号，因此方法的价值应谨慎表述为“可靠性敏感分类”场景下的效率改进，而非全新的覆盖率定理或具有决定性优势的剪枝规则。

Abstract: Split conformal prediction, not the pruning rule, supplies finite-sample marginal coverage once a pruned model is fixed independently of the conformal calibration split. We study the separate efficiency problem: can pruning preserve score geometry well enough to obtain smaller valid prediction sets? Calibration-Preserving Pruning (CPP) augments a base pruning score with nonconformity-gradient saliency and uses disjoint pruning, validation-selection, conformal-calibration, and test splits. Bounded score perturbations imply bounded conformal-quantile shifts and controlled set inflation, but do not make the generic coverage theorem CPP-specific. Final five-seed Qwen2.5-1.5B results at 50\% sparsity show the largest gains on large-label tasks. On DBpedia-14, CPP-SparseGPT reduces mean set size from \(10.1\) to \(8.6\) while changing accuracy from \(0.347\) to \(0.366\); CPP-Wanda reduces \(11.2\) to \(9.0\) with an accuracy trade-off from \(0.310\) to \(0.295\). Across 15 dataset--sparsity cells, CPP-SparseGPT produces smaller sets in 13 and higher accuracy in 11. Matched controls show that generic supervised gradients explain much of the gain: true-label CPP is not statistically resolved from matched Wanda+SNIP, whereas threshold-aware candidate-label CPP reaches \(7.8\) mean set size at explicit accuracy and offline-compute costs. RoBERTa-base and Llama-3-8B diagnostics support transfer, but our claims remain limited to reliability-sensitive classification.

</details>


### [141] [Tight Majorizations and Convergence Rates of Nuclear Norm Minimization IRLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23765)
*Christian Kümmerle,Tomas Masak,Dominik Stöger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对低秩恢复中的约束核范数最小化，为迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）方法建立了精确的收敛速率分析，证明调和均值（harmonic-mean）权重算子是光滑核范数的有效全局二次上界（majorizer），且在幂平均权重族内最优，因而优于仅利用行或列空间信息的经典单侧重加权方案，并给出维度无关的局部线性收敛率及反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRLS方法是核范数最小化的自然求解途径，但其收敛速率以及权重算子在算法中扮演的角色此前一直缺乏深入理解，亟需严格的理论刻画。

Method: 提出一种新的光滑核范数majorization分析，证明调和均值权重算子构成有效的全局二次majorizer，并证明其在幂平均权重算子族中的最优性；在Schatten-1零空间性质下证明采用多种权重算子的IRLS算法具有全局线性收敛；对调和均值权重IRLS证明维度无关的局部线性收敛率；构造反例表明单侧权重算子无法普遍获得该收敛率。

Result: 调和均值权重IRLS算法可获得维度无关的局部线性收敛速率，而文献中主流的单侧权重算子IRLS在一般情况下无法达到该速率；数值实验在方阵、矩形及对抗初始化等问题上验证了理论结果，并展示调和均值重加权的实际优势。

Conclusion: 调和均值权重算子在理论收敛速率与数值性能上均优于传统单侧重加权方案，为IRLS方法在低秩恢复中的高效应用提供了坚实的理论支撑。

Abstract: Iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS) methods constitute a natural approach to nuclear norm minimization, but their convergence rates and the role of the weight operator have remained poorly understood. This paper establishes sharp convergence rates for IRLS methods for constrained nuclear norm minimization in low-rank recovery. A central ingredient is a new majorization analysis for the smoothed nuclear norm: we prove that the harmonic-mean weight operator defines a valid global quadratic majorizer. Furthermore, we show that this weight operator is optimal within the family of power-mean weights, clarifying why it improves over classical one-sided reweighting schemes that use only row- or column-space information. Under a Schatten-1 null space property, we prove global linear convergence of IRLS algorithms using a variety of weight operators, including the harmonic-mean weights. For IRLS with harmonic-mean weights, we prove a dimension-independent, locally linear convergence rate. We provide a counterexample showing that this dimension-independent local rate cannot in general be obtained for IRLS algorithms using one-sided weight operators, which predominate in the literature. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results and illustrate the practical advantage of harmonic-mean reweighting across square, rectangular, and adversarially initialized recovery problems.

</details>


### [142] [Disentangled Skill Representations for Predictive Human Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23776)
*Mariah Schrum,Deepak Gopinath,Srijan Srivatsa,Guy Rosman,Tiffany Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAIL（可解释潜变量的技能抽象）方法，将人类技能建模为可解释、多维度的持久性表征，从自然行为中推断。SAIL使用持久技能嵌入控制专家与新手基线的混合，并通过反事实子技能交换实现解耦训练，从而对瞬时表现波动具有鲁棒性。在赛车和棒球领域验证了强预测性能、行为学上更优的解耦效果及更好的AI教练下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类技能对于与人类协作、指导或辅助的AI系统至关重要。与依赖单次观测的典型潜变量估计不同，技能是一种持久、组合且以行为为根基的构念，必须从随时间变化的模式中推断，因此需要专门针对这一特性的建模方法。

Method: SAIL为每个个体学习一个持久的技能嵌入，该嵌入控制专家与新手基线的混合；采用反事实子技能交换（counterfactual subskill swaps）进行训练以实现解耦。这种设计同时鼓励表征对表现波动具有鲁棒性，并保持结构化、可解释的特性；技能嵌入还支持可迁移的子技能表征及技能信息化的行为预测。

Result: 在赛车和棒球数据集上，SAIL相比基线取得了强预测性能，并一致地提升了行为学上可解释的解耦（behaviorally grounded disentanglement）；同时改善了下游AI教练任务的性能。

Conclusion: SAIL证明，将技能作为持久、多维度潜变量并与反事实解耦训练相结合，能够获得鲁棒、可解释且可迁移的技能表征，为理解人类技能和构建面向协作/教练的AI系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Understanding human skill is important for AI systems that collaborate with, coach, or assist people. Unlike typical latent variable estimation problems which rely on single observations, skill is a persistent, compositional, and behaviorally grounded construct that must be inferred from patterns over time. We introduce Skill Abstraction with Interpretable Latents (SAIL), a method for modeling human skill as an interpretable, multi-dimensional construct inferred from naturalistic behavior. Our approach produces a skill embedding that is robust to transient performance fluctuations and learns a transferable representation of human subskills. Furthermore, SAIL supports skill-informed behavior prediction that generalizes across a variety of in-domain contexts. We represent each individual with a persistent skill embedding that controls a blend between expert and novice bases and is trained using counterfactual subskill swaps for disentanglement. This design encourages representations that are both robust to performance variation and structured for interpretability. We demonstrate across racing and baseball that SAIL achieves strong predictive performance and consistently improves behaviorally grounded disentanglement over the evaluated baselines, while also improving downstream AI coaching performance.

</details>


### [143] [GAP-Prompt: Gated Adaptive Prompting for Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23782)
*Trung-Anh Dang,Duy-Cuong Bui,Ngoc-Son Vu,Christel Vrain,Vincent Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAP-Prompt 是一种面向持续学习的门控自适应提示方法，通过实例级门控、动态知识融合和共享提示蒸馏三个协同模块，在冻结骨干网络上实现细粒度的自适应提示注入。在 CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R 和 CUB-200 基准上达到 SOTA，其中 CUB-200 上达到 87.29% 的准确率，逼近联合训练上限（88.00%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习的核心挑战是灾难性遗忘。虽然基于提示的预训练模型方法通过冻结骨干网络提供了有效方案，但现有方法大多采用静态的任务级提示策略，忽略了任务内更细粒度的类别多样性（即同一任务内不同样本的差异），因此缺乏实例级的自适应能力。

Method: 提出了三个协同模块：(1) 实例条件门控——为每张图片动态决定最优的提示注入层级；(2) 动态知识融合——按实例感知方式聚合当前与历史提示，实现跨任务知识整合；(3) 共享提示蒸馏——在早期共享层锚定基础知识以缓解遗忘。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R 和 CUB-200 基准上的广泛评估显示 GAP-Prompt 一致取得最先进性能。在细粒度 CUB-200 数据集上达到 87.29% 准确率，逼近联合训练上界（88.00%），并显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: GAP-Prompt 表明将提示策略从任务级细化为实例级能够更好地捕捉任务内多样性，在提升新任务适应性的同时通过提示蒸馏有效缓解灾难性遗忘，逼近联合训练性能上界。实例级自适应是提升持续学习中提示方法性能的关键方向。

Abstract: Continual learning faces the persistent challenge of catastrophic forgetting, where sequential task updates degrade previously acquired knowledge. While prompt-based methods integrated with pre-trained models offer a compelling solution by freezing the backbone, they often rely on static, task-level prompting strategies that overlook fine-grained intra-task diversity. In this paper, we propose Gated Adaptive Prompting (GAP-Prompt), a novel method that introduces instance-level adaptability to the prompting process. GAP-Prompt consists of three synergistic modules: (1) instance-conditioned gating, which dynamically determines optimal prompt injection layers for each individual image; (2) dynamic knowledge fusion, which performs instance-aware aggregation of current and historical prompts, enabling knowledge integration across tasks; and (3) shared prompt distillation, which anchors foundational knowledge in early shared layers to mitigate forgetting. Extensive evaluations on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-R, and CUB-200 benchmarks demonstrate that GAP-Prompt consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, on the fine-grained CUB-200 dataset, GAP-Prompt reaches 87.29% accuracy, approaching the joint training upper bound (88.00%) and outperforming existing methods by a significant margin.

</details>


### [144] [Mixture of Channel Experts: Static Sparse Supports with Input-Adaptive Mixing for Pointwise Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23794)
*Elian Iluk,Gil Ben-Artzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将专家混合（MoE）的思路从“算子复制”迁移到“通道选择”，提出混合通道专家（MoCE）层。与并行卷积专家学到几乎相同滤波器不同，MoCE将每个专家定义为单个输出通道，该通道只稀疏地支持k个输入通道，并通过逐输入预测的温度的softmax在均值型和最大值型聚合之间切换；残差专家和负载均衡损失保证通道覆盖完整。MoCE以k/C的相对成本替代二次成本的稠密1x1投影，在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-100、迁移学习、EfficientViT等实验中匹配或超越稠密基线，减少16.7%的MACs和端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出直接将MoE设计复制到卷积网络会因结构原因失败：并行卷积专家读取相同输入通道时学到几乎相同的滤波器。因此需要把专家轴从算子重复改为通道选择，在保持稀疏路由思想的同时，设计适合CNN的专家机制来降低通道混合的计算成本。

Method: 提出MoCE层：用稀疏通道混合替换1x1通道降维投影。每个专家是单个输出通道，学习k<C个输入通道的稀疏支持集；由逐输入预测温度（temperature）的softmax组合所选通道，使专家可在均值型与最大值型聚合间切换；增加残差专家汇总未被选中的通道，并用负载均衡损失维持各通道被选用的覆盖完整性。计算相对成本由C^2降为k/C，并已验证实际墙钟时间节省。

Result: 在多种设置（ResNet骨干上的ImageNet-1K和CIFAR-100、迁移学习、EfficientViT、现代训练配方）中，MoCE匹配或超越稠密基线和此前通道选择方法，同时减少16.7%的MACs并降低端到端延迟。

Conclusion: MoCE证明了一种将MoE思想适配到CNN的有效路径：不复制专家算子，而是让专家负责通道子集的选择与聚合，从而实现结构稀疏、计算高效且不损失精度（甚至更优）的通道混合层。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales language models by routing each input through a small set of independently parameterized experts. We show that copying this design into convolutional networks fails for a structural reason: parallel convolutional experts that read the same input channels learn nearly identical filters. We therefore move the expert axis from operator duplication to channel selection. We introduce Mixture of Channel Experts (MoCE), a structured sparse channel-mixing layer, inspired by MoE, that replaces pointwise (1x1) channel-reduction projections. In MoCE, an expert is a single output channel with a learned sparse support of k << C input channels. The selected channels are combined by a softmax whose temperature is predicted per input, so each expert can move between mean-like and max-like aggregation. A residual expert summarizes the unselected channels, and a load-balancing loss keeps channel coverage complete. MoCE replaces a dense projection whose cost is quadratic in C with a mechanism whose relative cost scales as k/C, and the predicted savings hold in measured wall-clock time. Across ResNet backbones on ImageNet-1K and CIFAR-100, transfer learning, EfficientViT, and a strong modern training recipe, MoCE matches or exceeds dense baselines and prior channel-selection methods while reducing MACs by 16.7% and end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [145] [A Theory of Speciation in Generative Diffusion Models on Compact Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23798)
*Alessio Marta,Paola Causin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成扩散模型中“物种形成”（speciation）现象的内禀理论，将物种形成刻画为演化概率密度临界点的分岔，而非传统理论所假设的对称叉形分岔或高维空间假设。利用谱热核表示显式刻画流形几何的角色，借助Poincaré-Hopf和Morse理论给出分数平衡点数量与类型的全局约束。对热核混合分布证明了典型物种形成事件具有一维临界核且服从A2折叠规范型；叉形分岔与多方向同时转变源于非典型的对称构型。文中给出了双峰混合与黎曼正单纯形的几何依赖的物种形成时间估计，建立了非退化折叠在分数扰动下的结构稳定性，并证明一阶时间位移仅由沿临界方向的分数误差分量决定。理论在球面上的von Mises-Fisher混合分布上得到验证，展示了叉形/鞍结分岔、拓扑模式与层次化多物种形成；最后，基于神经网络的图表式内禀分数学习方案在原型与更复杂数据集上对比了理论预测的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于扩散模型中物种形成的理论通常将其等同于对称叉形分岔，并假设工作于大维空间，这无法刻画真实情形中几何与拓扑的作用，也缺乏一般性。作者希望超越这些局限，建立一种不依赖维度假设、直接内禀于数据流形几何的理论框架，以理解去噪过程中初始无差别的轨迹如何逐步承诺到不同数据类别。

Method: 以流形上演化概率密度的临界点分岔来表征物种形成；采用谱热核表示显式体现流形几何；利用Poincaré-Hopf定理与Morse理论施加全局拓扑约束。对热核混合分布证明典型（generic）分岔对应一维临界核并推出A2折叠规范型，同时说明叉形和同步多方向转变来自非典型对称构型。推导出双峰混合与黎曼正单纯形的几何依赖物种形成时间估计；通过结构稳定性分析和一阶时间位移公式刻画分数扰动的效应。理论结合球面上von Mises-Fisher混合分布的数值实验，以及基于神经网络的图表式内禀分数学习方案进行验证。

Result: 证明典型物种形成事件具有一维临界核并服从A2折叠正常形；叉形与多方向同步转变仅在对称性非典型构型下出现。获得受流形几何影响的物种形成时间估计，并证明非退化折叠的结构稳定性及一阶时间位移仅由临界方向上的分数误差决定。球面实验观测到叉形分岔、鞍结分岔、拓扑模式与层次化多物种形成，且神经网络分数学习在原型和复杂数据集上对比验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一个将物种形成与流形几何、全局拓扑相联系的统一内禀理论，表明物种形成的形态（折叠、叉形、多方向）取决于构型的对称性，而临界核维度、临界点数量与物种形成时间均受流形拓扑与几何约束。该理论为理解扩散模型去噪过程中的类别承诺机制提供了更一般化、维度无关的数学工具，并可直接指导分数模型的几何感知学习。

Abstract: Speciation in generative diffusion models denotes the emergence of distinct stable branches during denoising, through which initially undifferentiated trajectories progressively commit to different data classes. In this work we develop an intrinsic theory of speciation for diffusion models supported on compact Riemannian manifolds: the aim is to go beyond existing theoretical descriptions, which usually identify speciation with a symmetric pitchfork bifurcation and assume to work in a large-dimensional space. We characterize speciation by bifurcations of the critical points of the evolving probability density. A spectral heat-kernel representation makes explicit the role of the manifold geometry, while Poincaré-Hopf and Morse theory impose global constraints on the number and type of score equilibria and reveal topologically-imposed geometrical modes. For mixtures of heat kernels, we prove that generic speciation events have a one-dimensional critical kernel and admit an A2 fold normal form; pitchforks and simultaneous multidirectional transitions arise from nongeneric symmetric configurations. We derive geometry-dependent estimates of speciation times for bimodal mixtures and Riemannian regular simplices. We further establish structural stability of nondegenerate folds under score perturbations and show that the first-order time shift is determined solely by the component of the score error along the critical direction. The theory is illustrated on the sphere using mixtures of von Mises-Fisher distributions, where pitchfork and saddle-node bifurcations, topological modes, and hierarchical multiple speciations are observed. Finally, a chart-based intrinsic score-learning scheme based on neural networks contrasts the theoretically predicted transitions on prototypal and more complex datasets.

</details>


### [146] [Discovering Cross-Language Reasoning Invariance in LLMs with Geometry-Invariant Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23809)
*Igor Bogdanov,Changcheng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用5个模型（来自4个家族）在MGSM多语言小学数学数据集上研究“多语言数学问题解答是否依赖共享特征”。先用CKA识别跨语言对齐层，再在该层训练基线SAE和新提出的GI-SAE（带InfoNCE对比损失的稀疏自编码器），并通过在语言间交换特征值、测量KL散度来检验特征的功能可互换性。结果表明：更高的几何相似度（CKA/Jaccard）并不总是意味着更强的功能可互换性；跨语言特征共享因模型/架构而异，出现深度不同；GI-SAE主要放大模型中已存在的跨语言结构，效果因模型而异（Qwen增强、Gemma无功能收益、Llama/Phi呈分层混合效应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型能用不同语言解答同一数学问题，但不清楚其内部是靠跨语言共享特征，还是靠语言特有计算最终产生相似的输出。澄清这一点有助于理解多语言模型的内在计算机制。

Method: 使用MGSM数据集，在英文、德文、法文、西文、俄文、中文6种语言下解题，筛出6种语言均有有效推理轨迹的问题并回放记录多层表示。对每个模型先用CKA定位跨语言对齐层，然后在每层训练两个SAE：基线重构SAE和本文新提出的GI-SAE（在重构损失外加入InfoNCE损失，使同一问题的轨迹在不同语言和token位置上激活相似）。最后通过在模型前向传播时跨语言交换特征值，用KL散度量化每个特征造成的输出变化，检验共享特征的功能可互换性。

Result: GI-SAE在几乎所有层都取得更高的CKA和Jaccard相似度，但更高的几何相似度并不一致地带来更强的功能可互换性。跨语言特征共享在该样本中因模型/架构而异，且出现在不同模型的不同深度。GI-SAE主要放大已存在的跨语言结构：Qwen中增强，Gemma无功能收益，Llama和Phi呈分层依赖的混合效应。

Conclusion: 多语言模型解决同一数学问题时，其表示中既有共享特征也有语言特有计算，且共享程度与深度高度依赖于模型架构。几何层面的对齐（CKA/Jaccard）不能可靠预测功能层面的可互换性，GI-SAE的作用是放大而非创造跨语言共性，提示未来应结合功能测试来评估可解释性特征，而非仅依赖几何相似度指标。

Abstract: Multilingual language models can solve the same mathematical problem in different languages, but it remains unclear whether they rely on shared features or on language-specific computations that only produce similar outputs. We study this question in five models from four families using the Multilingual Grade School Math (MGSM) dataset, with problems solved in English, German, French, Spanish, Russian, and Chinese, retaining problems with valid reasoning traces in all six languages and replaying those traces through the model to record representations at multiple layers. For each model, we first use Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) to identify layers with cross-language alignment. At each selected layer, we train two sparse autoencoders (SAE): a baseline reconstruction-only model and a contrastive variant introduced in this work, the Geometry-Invariant SAE (GI-SAE). GI-SAE supplements the reconstruction loss with an Information Noise-Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) loss that trains the encoder to produce similar activations for traces of the same problem, regardless of language or token position. We then test whether the resulting shared features are functionally interchangeable by swapping their values between languages during the model's forward pass and measuring the resulting change in output, quantified by Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence per feature. Although GI-SAE yields higher CKA and Jaccard similarity at nearly every layer, higher geometric similarity does not consistently imply greater functional interchangeability. We find that cross-language feature sharing is model- and architecture-dependent in this sample and appears at different depths in different models. GI-SAE primarily amplifies cross-language structure already present: the pattern is model-specific, with strengthening in Qwen, no functional benefit in Gemma, and mixed layer-dependent effects in Llama and Phi.

</details>


### [147] [Learning to Grade Efficiently: A Bandit-Driven Prompt-Selection Framework for Low-Cost LLM Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23814)
*Olga Manakina,Igor Bogdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多臂老虎机（MAB）控制器的成本感知方法，在推理过程中自适应选择最优提示词策略用于作文自动评分（AES）。在雅思写作Task 2数据集上，该方法在保持与穷举网格搜索相当评分精度的同时，将LLM调用次数减少78.4%。实验评估了四种评分方案（多步vs单步、含/不含校准示例），发现多步且含示例的方案准确率最高，并首次生成了作文评分的成本-可靠性学习曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM作文自动评分方法普遍采用固定的提示词选择策略，既未考虑运营成本问题，也无法适应推理过程中不断变化的最优配置。提示词选择本质上是离线超参数优化问题，缺乏高效、动态的在线调整机制。

Method: 将每种提示词类型视为多臂老虎机（MAB）控制器中的一个臂（arm），在推理阶段通过在线学习自适应地选择最优提示词策略。实现并比较了四种评分方案：多步vs单步评估、含/不含校准示例，同时追踪token使用量、延迟及一致性指标以构建成本-可靠性学习曲线。

Result: MAB框架在雅思写作Task 2作文上达到与穷举网格搜索相当的评分精度，同时将寻找最佳评分方法所需的LLM调用减少78.4%。四种方案中，含校准示例的多步评估方案准确率最高。

Conclusion: 该研究是首个将在线控制机制用于AES中自适应选择提示词策略的工作，将提示词选择从离线超参数优化问题转变为高效的在线学习任务，为需要在运营成本与评估有效性之间权衡的教育技术平台提供了可操作洞见。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated essay scoring (AES), but contemporary approaches typically employ fixed prompt selection, failing to address operational cost concerns and evolving optimal configurations. We propose a novel cost-aware approach that treats each prompt type as an arm in a multi-armed bandit (MAB) controller, enabling adaptive selection of optimal prompting strategies during inference. Our experiments on IELTS Writing Task 2 essays show that the MAB framework achieves comparable scoring accuracy to exhaustive grid search while reducing LLM calls by 78.4\% to find the best grading approach. We implemented four distinct grading recipes (multi-step vs. single-step assessment, with vs. without calibration examples) and found that the multi-step approach with examples achieves the highest accuracy. By tracking token usage and latency alongside agreement metrics, we produce the first cost-reliability learning curves for essay scoring, providing actionable insights for educational technology platforms that must balance operational costs against assessment validity. This work represents the first application of online control mechanisms to adaptively select prompting strategies in AES, transforming prompt selection from an offline hyperparameter optimization problem into an efficient online learning task.

</details>


### [148] [AQLoRA: A Zero-Search Recipe for Fast Quantized LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23816)
*Md Romyull Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AQLoRA 是一种在 QLoRA 基础上的自适应量化微调方案：仅需一次 CPU 遍历权重，按 NF4 重建误差对层排序，在内存预算内将 Top-K 层保留为 fp16（跳过反量化）以换取训练加速。速度设置加速约 11.1%，质量设置加速约 4.8% 且精度与 QLoRA 持平。论文还通过受控实验和重复整轮会话的计时经验，揭示了"受保护层的数量而非身份"决定加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLoRA 虽然省内存但并未节省时间：它在每次前/反向传播中都要对每个 4-bit 权重进行动态反量化（dequantization），因此训练速度比 fp16 LoRA 更慢。本文旨在找回这部分时间开销，且不依赖搜索或校准数据。

Method: 一次 CPU 遍历所有权重即可完成配置：按 NF4 重建误差对各层排序，在给定内存预算下将误差最大的 Top-K 层保留为 fp16，从而跳过反量化以获得加速。提供两种设置：质量设置（quality）逐层自适应；速度设置（speed）仅自适应顶层块，使反向传播提前终止（early stopping）。该规则可在数秒内复现 Unsloth 手工挑选的动态 4-bit 层选择，而基于搜索的方法需要多轮校准。论文还给出在共享硬件上计时的三条规则：固定测量时长而非步数；用重复的分支臂测量噪声底而非近似方法；重复整轮会话（用一轮 sweep 内部的噪声底会低估真实不确定性数倍，随机种子几乎无法控制它）。

Result: 在 Commonsense-170K 上、六个模型、四个架构族（1.4B–14B）进行评估：速度设置比调优的 QLoRA 快 11.1% ± 2.7%，代价约 1 个精度点；在全部 9 次独立计时会话中均更快，最差也有 7% 的提升。质量设置快 4.8% ± 2.4%，在每个模型上精度与 QLoRA 持平，且与 fp16 LoRA 相差 1 个点以内，仅多占 0.2 GiB 内存。误差棒来自独立会话之间而非单次会话内部。受控实验证明两个方案失败：按权重密度选择适配层、按量化误差选择受保护层均不比随机选择更好；加速效果来自受保护层的数量而非其身份。

Conclusion: AQLoRA 以近乎零成本（一次 CPU pass、无搜索、无校准数据）换来 QLoRA 可观的训练加速，质量/速度两档设置可在精度与速度之间权衡；同时，计时方法论（重复整轮会话以测真实不确定性）和受控实验结论共同表明：在混合精度 LoRA 中，受保护层的数量决定速度收益，其具体选层身份并非关键。

Abstract: Quantized fine-tuning (QLoRA) saves memory but not time. It dequantizes every 4-bit weight on the fly, so it trains more slowly than fp16 LoRA. We present AQLoRA (Adaptive-Quantization LoRA), a recipe that buys part of that time back. One CPU pass over the weights sets everything, with no search and no calibration data. The pass ranks layers by NF4 reconstruction error and keeps the top-K in fp16 under a memory budget. Those layers skip dequantization, which is where the speed comes from. A quality setting adapts every layer. A speed setting adapts only the top blocks, so the backward pass stops early. The rule reproduces Unsloth's hand-curated dynamic-4bit selection exactly, in seconds, where search-based allocation needs repeated calibration passes.
  We evaluate on Commonsense-170K across six models and four architecture families, from 1.4B to 14B. The speed setting trains 11.1 +/- 2.7% faster than well-tuned QLoRA and gives up about one accuracy point. It was faster in all nine independent timing sessions, at worst by 7%. The quality setting trains 4.8 +/- 2.4% faster. Its accuracy is level with QLoRA on every model and within a point of fp16 LoRA, for 0.2 GiB more memory. These error bars are measured between independent sessions, not within one. Earning them taught us three rules for timing on shared hardware. Fix the measurement duration, not the step count. Measure the noise floor from a duplicated arm, not a nearly identical method. Repeat whole sessions: a floor computed inside one sweep understates the real uncertainty several times over, and the random seed controls almost none of it.
  We validate the recipe with controls and report the two that failed. Choosing adapter layers by weight density is no better than random. Choosing protected layers by quantization error is not either. The count of protected layers, not their identity, carries the speed effect.

</details>


### [149] [Generating Intervention Hypotheses using Explainable Explanations on Graphs: G2I, a Two-Stage Greedy Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23835)
*Mulin Tian,Ajitesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将反事实解释重构为干预设计问题，提出一种面向图神经网络的可操作反事实解释方法：局部层面通过贪心搜索直接生成最小、可操作的节点特征与邻居条件修改，并转化为可解释规则；网络层面将干预选择建模为带预算约束的DNF覆盖问题（非递减、近似子模），可用贪心算法获取理论保证。在合成图与真实自杀风险网络上验证了其可扩展性、成本效益及相比基于掩码反事实方法（如CF-GNNExplainer、CF²）的显著效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的公共卫生和社会科学决策需要预测模型，但将预测转化为有效干预必须解释模型行为。现有GNN解释方法多为节点级，不支持可操作的网络级干预设计；现有反事实GNN解释器（如CF-GNNExplainer、CF²）依赖连续掩码优化，隐含可随意操作边的假设、可能将计算开销分配给不可变或不可操作的属性、计算开销大，且解释过程本身难以被非AI专家的领域专家理解。作者因此提出疑问：简单方法能否生成好的解释？

Method: 将反事实解释重构为干预设计问题。局部层面：用贪心搜索直接识别节点特征和邻居级条件的最小、可操作改动，推导贪心搜索提供保证的条件并在经验上验证其近似满足；随后将反事实转化为适于现实干预的可解释规则。网络层面：将干预选择形式化为带预算约束的DNF（析取范式）覆盖问题，该问题非递减且近似子模，从而可用贪心算法获得理论保证。

Result: 在合成图和真实自杀风险网络上的实验表明，该方法能够产生可扩展、成本有效的干预策略，相比基于掩码的反事实方法（如CF-GNNExplainer、CF²）在效率上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 研究证明简单、可解释的贪心方法足以生成高质量的GNN反事实解释，既解决了既有掩码方法在可操作性、可解释性与计算开销上的不足，又通过局部贪心搜索和全局DNF覆盖优化的结合，提供了从节点级反事实到网络级干预策略的完整、有理论保证的框架。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making in public health and social science can greatly benefit from predictive models, yet translating predictions into effective interventions requires explaining the model behavior. While Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are well-suited for modeling relational data, existing explanation methods largely operate at the node level and fall short of supporting actionable, network-level intervention design. Existing counterfactual GNN explainers, such as CF-GNNExplainer and CF$^2$, rely on continuous mask optimization over features and edges, which implicitly assume feasible edge manipulation, may allocate effort to immutable or non-actionable attributes, and incur substantial computational overhead. Further, the method of arriving at the explanation itself is difficult to explain to a domain specialist who is not an AI expert. Can simple methods generate good explanations? To explore this, we reframe counterfactual explanation as an intervention design problem. At the local level, we generate counterfactuals via a greedy search that directly identifies minimal, actionable changes to node features and neighbor-level conditions. We derive conditions under which the greedy search provides guarantees, and empirically show that these conditions are approximately met. These counterfactuals are converted into interpretable rules suitable for real-world intervention. At the network level, we formulate intervention selection as a Disjunctive Normal Form (DNF) coverage problem under a budget constraint, which is nondecreasing and approximately submodular, enabling a greedy algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on synthetic graphs and real-world suicide risk networks demonstrate that our approach produces scalable, cost-effective intervention strategies with significantly improved efficiency over mask-based counterfactual methods.

</details>


### [150] [PuzzleKV: Page-Wise Low-Rank Decomposition for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23843)
*Zizhong Wang,Jieying Wang,Zhao Zhang,Jiajia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PuzzleKV 是一种面向长上下文 LLM 推理的无训练、无校准的 KV 缓存压缩方法。它将每个注意力头中的 KV 缓存按固定长度划分为“逻辑页”，观察到每一页内部存在显著的低秩结构，并以每个已完成的页作为独立压缩单元进行分解。在相同存储预算下（约占原始缓存 60%），PuzzleKV 可在多个模型和基准上达到 Full KV 96% 以上的性能；与量化结合后仅用 18.7% 存储即可保留 93% 以上性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中 KV 缓存的内存占用日益成为瓶颈。现有低秩压缩方法要么从模型权重或校准激活构造固定投影空间，要么在较广的缓存区域上构造共享基，这些固定或共享表示可能无法捕捉细粒度但重要的信息，因此需要一种更能保留细节的压缩方案。

Method: 将每个注意力头的 KV 缓存划分为固定长度的逻辑页，观察到单页内存在显著低秩结构；以“已完成的页”为独立压缩单元，对每层、每个注意力头内的页分别进行分解（low-rank factorization）；计算注意力时直接同时使用稠密（未压缩）页与分解后的因子化页，并在自回归解码过程中对新增的合格页进行增量压缩。方法无需训练、无需校准。

Result: 在多个模型、上下文长度和基准上的匹配存储预算实验中：约 60% 原始存储时，PuzzleKV 在两个评估模型及所有基准设置上达到 Full KV 的 96% 以上性能，在 RULER 上明显优于 Global SVD，在 LongBench 上具有竞争力；结合量化后仅用 18.7% 存储仍能保留 93% 以上的 Full KV 性能。

Conclusion: 以页为粒度的独立低秩分解能更好地保留细粒度信息，在相同存储预算下优于全局共享基的低秩压缩（如 Global SVD），并可进一步与量化组合实现更激进的压缩率，是一种有效、通用且无需训练的 KV 缓存压缩途径。

Abstract: Long-context inference in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly limited by the memory required for the key-value (KV) cache. KV cache compression addresses this problem by reducing the storage cost of previous tokens. Among existing approaches, low-rank compression is particularly attractive because it represents every token in reduced dimensions. Previous low-rank methods typically derive fixed projection spaces from model weights, construct fixed spaces from calibration activations, or construct a shared basis over a broad cache region. Such representations may not capture detailed but important information. We partition each per-head KV cache into fixed-length logical pages and observe substantial low-rank structure within individual pages. Based on this observation, we propose PuzzleKV, a training- and calibration-free method that treats each completed page as an independent compression unit. PuzzleKV decomposes pages within each layer and KV head, computes attention directly over dense and factorized pages, and incrementally compresses newly eligible pages during autoregressive decoding. Experiments across models, context lengths, and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of PuzzleKV under matched storage budgets. At approximately 60% of the original KV cache storage, PuzzleKV achieves more than 96% of Full KV performance across both evaluated models and all benchmark settings, with substantial gains over Global SVD on RULER and competitive performance on LongBench. To achieve a more aggressive compression ratio, PuzzleKV can be further combined with quantization while retaining more than 93% of Full KV performance using only 18.7% of the original storage.

</details>


### [151] [FlowNeg: GFlowNet-Guided Diverse Hard Negative Sampling for Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23849)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Naoshin Anzum Hridi,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowNeg 是一个基于上下文条件的层次化生成流网络（GFlowNet），用于知识图谱嵌入（KGE）的负采样：给定正三元组和损坏侧，先生成类型、再生成实体，从而在不归一化复合奖励的情况下实现与奖励成比例的采样。其终端奖励结合了模型硬度（有界）与仅训练阶段使用的结构分数（用于避免与保留的正样本冲突）。作者推导了奖励公式，特化了轨迹平衡（Trajectory Balance）目标，并给出了残差不平衡对终端概率和模式概率的乘法界。在5种架构×5个基准的5种子网格中，FlowNeg 的平均 MRR 在24/25个单元中高于 EMU 和 IF-NS；在 FB15k-237/RotatE 的15种子控制实验中（匹配负样本数量、诊断预算和计算），FlowNeg 达到 0.359±0.001 MRR，优于 EMU 的 0.346±0.002，且具有近均匀的固定分区多样性、高梯度信息量和低冲突率。结论支持模式覆盖的负样本生成，同时不将结构相似度视为开放世界的真实标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有负采样方法存在两难：均匀负采样多样但过于简单（无信息量），而难负样本挖掘（hard-negative mining）聚焦于少量实体，容易与保留的（held-out）正三元组发生冲突，损害评估可信度。此外，基于奖励的采样通常需要对实体集上的复合奖励做归一化，计算代价高。作者希望设计一种既能覆盖多种模式（mode-covering）、又能避免与真正三元组碰撞的高效负采样器。

Method: FlowNeg 是上下文条件的层次化生成流网络：给定正三元组和损坏侧，依次选择类型（type）再选择实体（entity）。其终端奖励由两部分组成——有界的基于模型硬度的得分（bounded model-based hardness）以及仅用于训练的结构得分（用于惩罚与保留正样本的碰撞），支持集为关系特定的类型兼容实体集。训练采用特化的轨迹平衡（trajectory balance）目标；理论上给出残差不平衡对终端概率与模式概率的乘法有界影响。

Result: 在5种架构×5个基准的5种子网格中，FlowNeg 的均值 MRR 在24/25个单元中优于 EMU 和 IF-NS（平均分别 +0.0172 和 +0.0160）。在 FB15k-237/RotatE 的15种子控制实验（固定负样本数量、诊断预算和计算量）中，FlowNeg 达到 0.359±0.001 MRR，显著高于 EMU 的 0.346±0.002，并展现出近均匀的固定分区多样性、高梯度信息量和低碰撞率。

Conclusion: 证据支持模式覆盖的负样本生成（mode-covering negative generation）这一方向：在不把结构相似性当作开放世界中的真实三元组（truth oracle）的前提下，可以同时获得高信息量、多样性以及低冲突的负样本，从而提升 KGE 的链接预测性能与评估可靠性。

Abstract: Negative sampling determines whether a knowledge graph embedding (KGE) model learns from informative counterexamples or wastes updates on implausible corruptions. Uniform negatives are diverse but easy, whereas hard-negative miners concentrate on few entities and collide more with held-out positives. We introduce FlowNeg, a context-conditioned hierarchical generative flow network that amortizes reward-proportional sampling without normalizing a composite reward over the entity set: given a positive triple and corruption side, it selects a type, then an entity. Its terminal reward combines bounded model-based hardness with a training-only structural score for held-out-positive collision, over a relation-specific type-compatible support. We derive the reward, specialize standard trajectory balance, and bound multiplicatively how residual imbalance perturbs terminal and mode probability. Across a descriptive five-seed grid of five architectures and five benchmarks, FlowNeg has higher mean MRR than EMU and than IF-NS in 24 of 25 cells ($+0.0172$ and $+0.0160$ on average). A separate 15-seed FB15k-237/RotatE control fixing negative count, diagnostic budget, and compute gives FlowNeg $0.359\pm0.001$ MRR against $0.346\pm0.002$ for EMU, with near-uniform fixed-partition diversity, high gradient informativeness, and low collision. The evidence supports mode-covering negative generation without treating structural similarity as an open-world truth oracle.

</details>


### [152] [UHI-Bench: Benchmarking Dual-Source Urban Heat Island Modeling Across Cities in Diverse Climate Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23857)
*Wanyun Ling,Chenxi Liu,Yi Xie,Aopu Xu,Zhuoqi Zeng,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UHI-Bench，这是首个面向双源城市热岛（UHI）建模的基准，整合动态气象与静态城市形态环境背景，采用统一的"信号-机制-迁移"框架，在20个城市、9个Köppen气候带、5个任务上评估了来自4个模型家族的20余个基线模型。研究发现没有统一最优模型，基础模型表现持续稳定；环境协变量总体上提升性能但效用因源与任务而异；跨城市迁移性更多由UHI机制重叠而非气候带相似性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHI在气候变化下加剧，其两种主要观测（地表温度LST-UHI与近地表气温AirT-UHI）捕捉物理上不同的城市热特征，但多数研究仅依赖单一数据源，混用二者会显著偏差人体热暴露的幅度与空间变异性。同时，准确的UHI建模需要动态气象驱动与静态城市形态特征，时空不兼容阻碍了二者的对齐；LST云遮挡和AirT站点稀疏进一步限制了双源建模，亟需面向跨气候城市的迁移研究。

Method: 构建UHI-Bench基准，整合动态与静态环境上下文，提出统一的"信号（signal）、机制（mechanism）、迁移（transfer）"框架，在20个城市和9个Köppen气候类别上定义5个任务，系统评估来自4个模型家族的20余个基线模型。

Result: 结果显示：没有任何模型在所有情况下统一最优，但基础模型（foundation models）保持了一贯的竞争力和稳定性；环境协变量总体上改善性能，但其效用在不同数据源和任务间存在差异；跨城市迁移性由UHI机制的相似重叠（而非气候带相似性）更好地解释。

Conclusion: 该基准数据集和标准化流程为城市热建模提供了实用指导，促进了气候数据公平性，并支持未来气候研究的进展。

Abstract: Urban heat islands (UHIs) are intensifying under climate change, exacerbating thermal exposure risks. Their two primary observations, land surface temperature UHI (LST-UHI) and near-surface air temperature UHI (AirT-UHI), capture physically distinct aspects of urban heat. However, most studies rely on a single source, and substituting one for the other can substantially bias the magnitude and spatial variability of human heat exposure. Accurate UHI modeling also requires dynamic meteorological drivers and static urban morphology features, but spatiotemporal incompatibilities hinder their alignment. Cloud gaps in LST observations and sparse AirT station networks further limit dual-source UHI modeling, motivating cross-city transfer across diverse climates. To bridge these gaps, we introduce UHI-Bench, the first UHI benchmark for dual-source UHI modeling that integrates dynamic and static environmental context. Following a unified signal, mechanism, and transfer framework, it evaluates over 20 baselines from four model families on five tasks across 20 cities and nine Köppen climate classes. Results show that no model is uniformly best, although foundation models remain consistently competitive and stable. Environmental covariates generally improve performance, but their utility varies across sources and tasks. Cross-city transferability is better explained by overlap in UHI regimes than by climate-zone similarity. With the dataset and standardized pipeline, our work provides practical guidance for urban heat modeling, promotes climate data equity, and supports future advances in climate research.

</details>


### [153] [Revelation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23860)
*Qinyou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"揭示控制（Revelation Control）"理论：在有代价的干预中，仅当被揭示的隐藏状态差异足以改变关键决策时才予以揭示，并将干预本身带来的生产性进步单独核算。该框架为学习系统定义了"决策充分揭示"与"揭示深度"，把静态贝叶斯精化嵌入状态依赖的延续价值中，给出精确的代价调整因子判定准则——仅当共享标量摘要的状态位于定价的停止/继续边界两侧时，附加的浅层坐标才是决策非冗余的；同时给出与目标无关的模型特定实例化协议，并证明有界停止翻转风险本身无法在无限制严重性下确保正期望效用。Qwen2.5-7B与Mistral-7B-v0.3上的实验显示，更深层的未来学习探针具有正决策价值，生产性复用带来严格相等算力下的效用优势；Qwen还展示了决策非冗余的浅层可揭示性区间，而Mistral中仅在独立开发面板上拟合的标量延续架构在不相交目标面板上仍保持正的族式调整下界，支持标量决策充分性。总体证据支持"结构性而非数值性迁移"：决策理论、成本核算、延续逻辑与评估协议可迁移，而经验代理、系数、阈值乃至所要求的浅层状态维度可能因系统而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的干预（如测试、训练、探查）既会揭示隐藏状态，也可能本身改变系统状态（产生生产性进展）。需要一种决策理论，区分"纯粹的信息价值"与"生产性复用"，避免为揭示而揭示——只有能改变关键决策的区分才值得付费，同时不忽略干预本身带来的效用。

Method: 提出决策充分的揭示（decision-sufficient revelation）与揭示深度（revelation depth）概念；将静态贝叶斯精化嵌入状态依赖的延续价值（continuation value）中，把纯信息价值与生产性复用分离；推导精确的代价调整因子判定准则（浅层坐标在何种条件下决策非冗余）；给出目标无关的模型特定实例化协议；并证明有界停止翻转风险无法单独证明正期望效用。实验在Qwen2.5-7B与Mistral-7B-v0.3上，用未来学习探针（future-learning probes）并对比相等计算预算下的生产性复用与无复用设置，且在独立开发/目标面板上做族式调整评估。

Result: 两种模型中，更深层未来学习探针具有正决策价值；生产性复用相比相等算力基线带来严格的效用优势。Qwen额外显示存在决策非冗余的浅层可揭示性区间；Mistral中仅靠开发面板拟合的标量延续架构在不相交目标面板上仍保持正的族式（familywise）调整下界，说明在该架构家族与分辨率下标量决策充分性成立。

Conclusion: 证据支持"结构性迁移而非数值性迁移"：决策理论、成本核算、延续逻辑与评估协议跨模型可迁移，而经验代理、系数、阈值乃至所需的浅层状态维度都可能因具体系统而异。这意味着该框架可作为通用的决策与成本核算理论，但具体实例化参数须按系统定制。

Abstract: Revelation Control is the problem of choosing priced interventions that reveal hidden state only insofar as the revealed distinctions can change a consequential decision, while accounting separately for any useful progress created by the intervention itself. We develop this theory for learning systems, where states equivalent under declared current information can respond differently to future training and favor different actions. The framework defines decision-sufficient revelation and revelation depth, separates pure information value from productive reuse, embeds static Bayes refinement into state-dependent continuation value, and gives an exact cost-adjusted factorization criterion: an additional shallow coordinate is decision-nonredundant only when states sharing a scalar summary lie on opposite sides of the priced Stop/Continue boundary. We also give a target-independent protocol for model-specific instantiation and prove that bounded stop-flip risk alone cannot certify positive expected utility under unrestricted severity. Across Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B-v0.3, deeper future-learning probes have positive decision value and productive reuse yields strict equal-compute utility advantages. Qwen additionally provides evidence for a decision-nonredundant shallow revealability regime; in Mistral, a scalar continuation architecture fit only on an independent development panel retains positive familywise-adjusted lower bounds on a disjoint target panel, consistent with scalar decision sufficiency within the tested architecture family and resolution. The evidence supports structural rather than numerical transfer: the decision theory, cost accounting, continuation logic, and evaluation protocol transport, while empirical proxies, coefficients, thresholds, and even the required shallow state dimension may be system-specific.

</details>


### [154] [Every Layer Counts: An Exponential $L_2$ Depth Hierarchy for ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23877)
*Itay Safran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明ReLU神经网络的深度层级定理：每增加一个ReLU层可指数级节省神经元个数。对任意ℓ≥3，存在一个全局[0,1]取值、1-Lipschitz的函数可由深度ℓ、宽度O(d^4)的网络实现；而任何深度ℓ-1、宽度至多2^d/[2d(ℓ-2)]的网络（权重完全不受限制）在某个绝对连续分布下的平方L2误差至少为1/24。这是浅层深度≥3的两个固定深度之间的首个指数分离，也是所有相邻固定深度上的首个指数层级；该下界还直接导出精确计算对应的层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度对ReLU网络表达能力/参数效率的根本影响，即深层网络能否用指数级更少的神经元取代浅层网络。此前相关工作或局限于深度2对3的分离，或对浅层权重/结构附加限制；Safran、Eldan与Shamir（2019）明确提出一个开放问题：在浅层网络权重不受限制的前提下，是否存在深度3对深度2的紧支撑指数分离。作者的目标是给出一般固定深度之间的指数层级并回答这一开放问题。

Method: 方法分为两部分：(1) 上界：显式构造一族高维函数，并给出宽度O(d^4)的深度ℓ网络实现；(2) 下界：利用组合/几何论证（区域计数或单元复杂度等技术）证明，任何宽度不超过指数量的深度ℓ-1网络（权重任意）在该函数上必然产生至少1/24的平方L2误差，从而得到近似层级并推论出精确计算层级；ℓ=3的构造给出紧支撑情形。此外，对更"良性"的函数给出精确分离：用单位超立方体上的逐点一致约束，证明任何深度3网络在第一隐藏层需要指数多个神经元才能精确匹配一个深度4多项式宽度网络可计算的函数。

Result: (1) 建立所有相邻固定深度（深度≥3）的指数近似层级与精确计算层级，每增加一层可指数节省神经元；(2) ℓ=3情形得到紧支撑、浅层权重不受限制的深度3对深度2分离，正面回答Safran等的问题，但其分布在指数半径处支撑质量，因而不落入会推出重大阈值电路下界的正则性区间；(3) 给出更良性函数的精确分离：全局[0,1]值、O(√d)-Lipschitz、把单位超立方体映满[0,1]，可由多项式宽度深度4网络计算，而任何单位超立方体上与其逐点一致的深度3网络第一隐藏层需指数多个神经元。

Conclusion: ReLU网络在任意两个固定深度（均≥3）之间存在首个指数分离，并在所有相邻固定深度上成立指数层级，且对权重不加任何限制；同时解决了Safran-Eldan-Shamir关于深度3对深度2非受限权重的开放问题。作者同时澄清这种构造相对于阈值电路下界的正则性局限（支撑半径指数大），为该方向后续工作划定了边界。

Abstract: We prove a depth hierarchy for ReLU neural networks in which every additional ReLU layer can save exponentially many neurons. For every $\ell\geq 3$, a globally $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz function is realized by a depth-$\ell$ network of width $\mathcal{O}(d^4)$, whereas every depth-$(\ell-1)$ network with unrestricted weights and width at most $2^d/[2d(\ell-2)]$ has squared $L_2$ error at least $1/24$ under an absolutely continuous distribution. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first exponential separation for ReLU networks between two fixed depths whose shallower depth is at least $3$, and the first exponential hierarchy across all adjacent fixed depths. The lower bound also immediately yields the corresponding hierarchy for exact computation. Moreover, the case $\ell=3$ gives a compactly supported depth-$3$-versus-depth-$2$ separation with unrestricted shallow-network weights, answering a question raised by Safran, Eldan, and Shamir (2019, Sec. 2.3). The corresponding distribution nevertheless has all its mass at exponential radius, so the construction falls outside the regularity regime in which such a separation would imply major threshold-circuit lower bounds. We also prove an exact separation for a more benign function. It is computed by a polynomial-width depth-$4$ network, whereas every depth-$3$ network agreeing with it on the unit hypercube requires exponentially many neurons in its first hidden layer, again without any restriction on the weights. The function is globally $[0,1]$-valued and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt d)$-Lipschitz, and maps the unit hypercube onto $[0,1]$.

</details>


### [155] [Partial Optimal Transport on the Circle for All Transported Masses in O(N log N)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23910)
*Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAWC算法，用于在圆周(圆/流形S^{d-1})上高效计算部分最优传输(Partial Optimal Transport)的完整配置(profile)。部分最优传输允许部分质量不匹配，能抵抗异常值、遮挡与杂波，而完整配置(所有运输基数的最优代价)是实际关心的量。此前圆上问题的朴素精确方法需在每个支撑间隙上运行一次线算法，复杂度为O(N²logN)；本文利用"无切口线结构"与"自由间隙不变量"证明该倍数N是不必要的，得到精确的O(NlogN)时间、O(N)内存的PAWC算法，一次性返回所有K+1个代价、嵌套活动集与计划，以及一个对所有基数同时最优的单一间隙。在大圆上切片可将它扩展到S^{d-1}。实验显示N=4096时完整配置仅0.56ms，而通用求解器仅单个运输分数需1.5s；在含遮挡/杂波的MPEG-7形状上，保持描述子固定仅改变代价，PAWC保留了66%的干净数据检索分数，而均衡圆OT仅为16%；在S²上对受污染目标，其拟合误差比球面切片Wasserstein减半。代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分最优传输因允许部分质量不匹配而对异常值、遮挡和杂波具有鲁棒性，实际应用中关心的量是完整配置(每个运输基数下的最优代价)，但运输量通常未知。在线性实数轴上PAWL算法能以O(NlogN)返回该配置；然而大量真实数据是周期性的(角度、相位、方向、一天中的时间、色调、大圆投影的方向等)。圆上的部分最优传输具有全局环流(相当于一个优化切口)，朴素精确法需对每个支撑间隙各运行一次线算法，代价为O(N²logN)。作者的目标是消除这多余的N因子，使圆上(及球面上)的完整部分OT配置也能达到O(NlogN)。

Method: 作者证明线的结构可以在"无切口(cut-free)"形式下保存下来。他们引入"自由间隙不变量(free-gap invariant)"，在每一步提供一个切口，使之前所有局部更新在该切口处仍然是合法的线更新。基于此构造PAWC算法：精确的O(NlogN)时间、O(N)内存算法，一次运行即返回所有K+1个代价、嵌套活动集和运输计划，以及一个对所有基数同时最优的单一间隙。通过在大圆上切片(slicing)，该方法被推广到高维球面S^{d-1}。

Result: 实验表明：N=4096时PAWC计算完整配置仅需0.56ms，而通用求解器仅单个运输分数就要1.5s(约三个数量级的加速)；在含遮挡和杂波的MPEG-7形状上，保持描述子固定、仅改变代价度量，PAWC保留了66%的干净数据检索分数，而均衡圆OT只有16%；在S²上合成与真实污染目标场景中，拟合误差比球面切片Wasserstein减半。代码已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: PAWC表明圆周/球面上的部分最优传输完整配置并不需要O(N²logN)，可以用与线性情形相同的O(NlogN)复杂度精确求解。这使周期性数据(角度、相位、方向、色调、时间等)的鲁棒输运匹配变得实用，并为球面与切片式部分OT提供了高效且更鲁棒的替代方案。作者认为冗余的N因子是不必要的，并通过不变量构造了同时对所有基数最优的单一切口。

Abstract: Partial optimal transport compares two measures while leaving part of the mass unmatched, which is what makes it robust to outliers, occlusion, and clutter. The quantity of interest is usually the whole profile - the optimal cost at every transported cardinality - because the right amount to transport is rarely known in advance, and on the real line the PAWL algorithm returns that profile in $O(N\log N)$. Much data is periodic rather than linear: angles, phases, orientations, time of day, hue, and every direction obtained by projecting onto a great circle. On the circle the same problem acquires a global circulation, or equivalently an optimized cut, which the naive exact method handles by running the line algorithm once per support gap, at $O(N^{2}\log N)$. We show that this factor $N$ is unnecessary. The line structure survives in cut-free form, and a free-gap invariant supplies, at every step, a cut at which all previous local updates remain valid line updates. This yields PAWC: an exact $O(N\log N)$ time, $O(N)$ memory algorithm returning all $K+1$ costs, nested active sets and plans in one run, together with a single gap that is simultaneously optimal for every cardinality. Slicing over great circles extends it to $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$. Empirically the whole profile costs $0.56$ms at $N=4096$ against $1.5$s for a single transported fraction from a general solver; on occluded, cluttered mpeg-7 shapes, holding the descriptor fixed and varying only the cost, it retains $66\%$ of the clean-data retrieval score against $16\%$ for balanced circular OT, and on $\mathbb{S}^{2}$ it halves the fitting error of spherical sliced Wasserstein against contaminated targets, synthetic and real. Code is available at https://github.com/mint-vu/Partial_Wasserstein_on_Circles.

</details>


### [156] [The Loss Floor of Denoising Score Matching: Fisher Geometry from Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23916)
*Avinash Raju,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对去噪分数匹配（DSM）的训练损失进行了精确的条件方差分解，证明其"不可约超额项"正是沿扩散轨迹积分的条件端点族的 Fisher–Rao 度量之迹，即噪声状态关于干净数据的互信息损失速率，从而将扩散隐空间中的信息几何识别为训练损失的内在组成，并指出不同噪声范围/加权的原始损失因包含不同的加性地板而不具备一致模型排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪分数匹配以条件分数为回归目标，而生成过程实际需要边际分数。虽然二者在总体层面共享同一极小化器，但在固定噪声状态下条件目标仍是随机变量，导致训练损失中存在不可约的超额项（损失地板）。作者希望精确刻画这一超额项的结构与几何意义，以理解扩散模型训练损失的固有组成，并解释训练几何与采样误差统计之间的差异。

Method: 在温和正则性假设下，作者对一般腐蚀核导出条件方差分解，证明超额损失精确等于条件端点族 Fisher–Rao 度量之迹沿扩散轨迹的积分；推导基于 Schrödinger 桥变分原理，将理想目标视为路径空间上超出相对熵的项；并证明该 Fisher 项正比于噪声状态关于干净数据互信息损失的速率。在 Gauss 情形中给出损失地板的闭式解，并分析高信噪比发散与数据信息维数的关系。

Result: 得到如下结果：(1) DSM 损失地板 = ∫ Fisher–Rao 度量之迹（条件方差分解）；(2) 该地板被分解为数据决定的"信息流"与腐蚀时间表/目标决定的"权重"之乘积；(3) Gauss 情形下获得地板闭式解，恢复连续时间目标的重参数化不变性，并将高信噪比发散与数据的信息维数关联；(4) 不同噪声范围或加权的原始损失因含不同加性地板而不能一致地对模型排名；(5) 训练中涉及的二阶几何与数值采样误差中的三阶条件统计量形成对比。

Conclusion: 本文建立了扩散训练目标的信息几何解释：去噪分数匹配中不可约的损失地板是条件端点族的 Fisher–Rao 度量，等价于互信息损失速率，因而是数据与腐蚀过程的内禀属性而非数值缺陷。该认识要求在不同噪声范围/加权间比较模型时剥离加性地板，同时提示训练损失所见的二阶几何与采样误差所依赖的三阶统计量需分开对待。

Abstract: Denoising score matching trains diffusion models by regressing onto a conditional score, although generation ultimately requires the marginal score. The two objectives share the same population minimizer, but the conditional target remains random at fixed noisy state and introduces an irreducible excess in the training loss. We isolate this excess and show that, for a general corruption kernel under mild regularity assumptions, it is exactly the trace of the Fisher--Rao metric of the conditional endpoint family, integrated along the diffusion trajectory. This gives an exact conditional-variance decomposition of the denoising objective and identifies the information geometry observed in diffusion latent spaces as an intrinsic component of the training loss. We derive the result from a Schr"odinger bridge variational principle, in which the ideal objective arises as excess path-space relative entropy. For corruption diffusions, the Fisher term is proportional to the rate at which the noisy state loses mutual information about the clean data, separating the loss floor into an information flow determined by the data and a weight determined by the corruption schedule and objective. In the Gaussian case, this yields a closed form for the floor, recovers reparametrization invariance of the continuous-time objective, and relates its high-SNR divergence to the information dimension of the data. Finally, we show that raw losses obtained with different noise ranges or weightings need not rank models consistently because they contain different additive floors, and contrast the second-order geometry seen by training with the third-order conditional statistics entering numerical sampling error.

</details>


### [157] [GATNextHop: A GAT for Shortest Path Routing with Cross-Topology Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23917)
*Chia-Hong Chou,Katerina Potika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GATNextHop 模型，探索图注意力网络（GAT）能否逼近最短路径算法并在不同网络拓扑间泛化。模型在合成图上训练，在互联网拓扑动物园的真实 ISP 网络上测试，并与 Dijkstra 算法在准确率、推理速度和泛化性上对比，量化学习式路由与经典路由的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最短路径算法（如 OSPF 使用的 Dijkstra/SPF）虽然能提供精确的路由解，但每当网络拓扑变化时都必须重新计算，在动态或大规模网络中严重限制可扩展性，因此需要探索可跨拓扑泛化的学习式路由启发方法。

Method: 提出 GATNextHop 模型，基于图注意力网络训练学习路由下一跳预测；在合成图上训练模型，并在互联网拓扑动物园的真实 ISP 网络结构上进行跨拓扑泛化测试，通过与 Dijkstra 算法对照来评估学习式方法的性能。

Result: 从准确率、推理速度和泛化能力三个维度评估 GNN 对比 Dijkstra 算法的表现，用于量化学习式路由与经典路由方法之间的权衡（精确性与可扩展性）。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在验证图神经网络能否近似最短路径并跨拓扑迁移，为大规模/动态网络场景下以学习式路由启发替代经典精确算法提供可行性与代价分析依据。

Abstract: Common shortest-path algorithms, such as Dijkstra's (SPF), that OSPF uses, provide exact routing solutions but must be recomputed for each network topology, limiting scalability in dynamic or large-scale networks. This paper proposes the GATNextHop model to determine whether a Graph Neural Network, namely the Graph Attention Network, can approximate shortest paths and generalize across topologies. By training on synthetic graphs and evaluating on real-world Internet Service Provider networks from the Internet Topology Zoo, we aim to benchmark our model's ability to learn routing heuristics that transfer across network structures. Performance will be evaluated in terms of accuracy, inference speed, and generalization, comparing the GNN against Dijkstra's algorithm to quantify trade-offs between learned and classical routing approaches.

</details>


### [158] [MnemoDyn: Learning Resting State Dynamics from 40K FMRI sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23936)
*Sourav Pal,Viet Luong,Hoseok Lee,Tingting Dan,Guorong Wu,Richard Davidson,Won Hwa Kim,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MnemoDyn，一个基于动力系统的静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）建模框架，在大约 40K 条涵盖多种公开/授权数据集的 rs-fMRI 序列上进行了大规模预训练。与主流基于 Transformer 的方法不同，MnemoDyn 采用跨脑区划分的多分辨率时间动态建模，具备计算高效、跨人群与扫描协议泛化能力强的特点。在重建质量上持续优于当前最先进的 Transformer 方法，且在小样本研究中表现优异，对神经影像学具有广泛的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 rs-fMRI 建模方案大多依赖 Transformer 骨干网络，而脑区动态本质上是一种时间演化系统，Transformer 并非最自然的归纳偏置。此外，已有模型在计算效率、跨人群/跨协议的泛化性以及小样本场景下的表现仍有不足。作者旨在利用大规模非专有的 rs-fMRI 数据预训练，构建一个计算高效、泛化能力强且能服务多样下游任务的动力系统模型。

Method: 作者将 rs-fMRI 建模视为一个动力系统问题，对经过脑区划分（parcellation）后的脑区时间序列进行多分辨率的时间动态建模，而非使用 Transformer 骨干。模型在大规模（约 40K 序列）异质数据集上进行预训练，涵盖多种公开和授权数据集，以学习跨人群与扫描协议稳定的动态表征。
