Result: MnemoDyn 在计算效率上优于当前方法，并在跨多样化人群与扫描协议上表现出优异的泛化能力；在与 SOTA Transformer 方法对比时，重建质量持续领先。模型在各类下游任务上表现良好，尤其在小样本研究上展现了显著有效性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在（非专有的）大规模 rs-fMRI 数据上进行预训练，能够获得高度可用的模型；动力系统 + 多分辨率时序建模的范式相较 Transformer 在效率与泛化上更具优势。MnemoDyn 对小样本研究的适用性为广泛采用静息态 fMRI 的神经影像学研究提供了重要启示。

Abstract: We present a dynamical-systems based model for resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI), trained on a dataset of roughly 40K rs-fMRI sequences covering a wide variety of public and available-by-permission datasets. While most existing proposals use transformer backbones, we utilize multi-resolution temporal modeling of the dynamics across parcellated brain regions. We show that MnemoDyn is compute efficient and generalizes very well across diverse populations and scanning protocols. When benchmarked against current state-of-the-art transformer-based approaches, MnemoDyn consistently delivers superior reconstruction quality. Overall, we find that with such large-scale pre-training on (non-proprietary) rs-fMRI datasets, we get a highly performant model for various downstream tasks. Our results also provide evidence of the efficacy of the model on small sample size studies which has implications for neuroimaging studies at large where resting state fMRI is a commonly acquired imaging modality.

</details>


### [159] [CoDrift: Compositional Drifting for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23939)
*Xiewei Ni,Ruofeng Mei,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CoDrift，一种基于动作空间运动场组合的生成式离线强化学习框架，将三个目标级场（条件场、边际场、价值场）组合为统一策略场，通过一次前向传播生成动作，在 OGBench 和 D4RL 的 73 个任务上取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习本质上是多目标的：策略既要与固定数据集的轨迹行为支持保持一致，又要优先选择高价值动作。已有方法通常将这些异构目标分开处理，缺少统一的组合方式。作者将各目标重新表述为动作空间中的运动场，从而能够通过场的组合直接融合异构学习目标。

Method: 提出 CoDrift 组合框架（受漂移模型启发），用于单步生成式策略学习。它将三个目标级场组合为统一策略场：条件场保持状态依赖的行为结构；边际场跨状态池化动作，在连续控制离线 RL 的"单正样本"场景中提供更稳定的生成信号；价值场将生成动作移向高价值区域。组合后的场被吸收进一个随机生成器中，部署时仅需单次前向传播即可生成动作。

Result: 在 OGBench 和 D4RL 的 73 个任务上，分别于离线和离线到在线两种设置下评估，CoDrift 与最先进方法相比表现更好，并在两种设置下均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 通过将离线 RL 的多个目标统一表述为可组合的运动场，CoDrift 能够有效融合保真度与价值优化目标，以单步生成方式获得稳健且具竞争力的策略性能，为离线到在线的连续控制策略学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning is intrinsically multi-objective: a policy must remain compatible with the behavioral support of a fixed dataset while preferentially selecting high-value actions. We recast these objectives in a common form by viewing each as an action-space motion field that specifies how generated actions should move. This perspective enables heterogeneous learning objectives to be combined directly through field composition. Inspired by drifting models, we propose CoDrift, a compositional framework for one-step generative policy learning. CoDrift combines three objective-level fields into a unified policy field. The conditional field preserves state-dependent behavioral structure, while the marginal field pools actions across states to provide a more stable generative signal in the single-positive-sample regime of continuous-control offline RL. The value field moves generated actions toward higher-value regions. The composed field is absorbed into a stochastic generator that produces an action with a single forward pass at deployment. We evaluate CoDrift on 73 tasks from OGBench and D4RL in both offline and offline-to-online settings. CoDrift compares favorably with state-of-the-art methods and achieves the best average rank in both settings.

</details>


### [160] [Low-Latency Activation-Regularized Sparse Neural Operators with Distillation Assistance Towards Real-Time Edge-Deployable Virtual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23987)
*William Howes,Farid Ahmed,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单步稀疏激活ReLU（SAR）层，替代传统多步脉冲神经元，用于虚拟传感中的时空物理场实时重建与预测。在基于trunk的NOMAD架构下，SAR相较VSN和LIF在"延迟-误差-能量"（LEE）综合指标上提升超5倍；结合合成知识蒸馏可将LEE再降2倍以上，并在Heat Exchanger数据集上将L2误差降低约2倍乃至近7倍，为神经形态/边缘设备的能效型虚拟传感提供了可对比的金标准框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟传感需为数字孪生和安全关键系统提供实时、高泛化、低延迟且节能的时空物理场重建。传统计算与数据驱动方法在边缘部署上面临泛化能力、延迟与能效瓶颈；神经算子精度好但依赖高功耗硬件；脉冲神经元虽有能效优势，但其代理梯度训练与多步脉冲机制带来了收敛性和延迟问题。因此需要一种兼具稀疏性、低延迟与事件计算兼容性的高效替代方案。

Method: 提出Sparse-Activation-ReLU（SAR）层：一种无需代理梯度训练的单步激活方案，促进激活稀疏性并兼容事件驱动计算。将其集成到基于trunk的NOMAD网络架构中；引入合成知识蒸馏以进一步压缩LEE指标；同时通过ReLU-based脉冲损失和图邻域阈值化改进VSN（可变脉冲神经元）模型。

Result: 在Latency-Error-Energy（LEE）综合指标上，SAR较VSN和LIF实现超5倍提升；合成知识蒸馏使LEE评分再降低2倍以上。在Heat Exchanger数据集上，改进后的VSN将L2误差分别降低2倍以上和近7倍，同时减少了脉冲次数与空间聚合开销。

Conclusion: 该工作为能效型虚拟传感提供了一套可朝向神经形态或边缘设备集成的新框架，可作为衡量未来基于稀疏性或脑启发脉冲设计在延迟、能量与误差方面性能的基准（金标准），推动虚拟传感向低功耗实时边缘部署演进。

Abstract: Virtual sensing enables digital twins and safety-critical systems to reconstruct and forecast spatial-temporal physics in real time. However, conventional computational and data-driven methods often face challenges in generalization, latency, and energy efficiency for edge deployment. Neural operators offer a promising alternative but remain reliant on power-intensive hardware. Spiking neurons and neuromorphic computing can improve efficiency, yet surrogate-gradient training and multi-step spiking introduce convergence and latency challenges. We propose the Sparse-Activation-ReLU (SAR) layer, a single-step alternative that promotes activation sparsity without surrogate-gradient training while remaining compatible with event-based computing. Within a trunk-based NOMAD architecture, SAR achieves over a fivefold improvement in the combined Latency-Error-Energy (LEE) metric compared with Variable Spiking Neuron (VSN) and Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF) implementations. We further analyze spiking entropy and feature usage and introduce synthetic knowledge distillation, reducing the LEE score by more than twofold. Finally, we improve VSN through a ReLU-based spiking loss and graph-neighbor thresholding. On the Heat Exchanger dataset, these approaches reduce L2 error by more than twofold and nearly sevenfold, respectively, while reducing spiking and spatial aggregation. Overall, the work presented is a step towards energy-efficient virtual sensing by providing an alternative framework that can be positioned towards neuromorphic or other edge device integration that can be a gold standard to compare latency, energy, and error performance for future efficient designs that are sparsity or brain-inspired spiking based.

</details>


### [161] [Revenge of Monosemanticity: Specialized Neurons Improve Data Efficiency in MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24007)
*Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Enric Boix-Adsera,Fivos Kalogiannis,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，关于神经网络特征学习的现有理论过度强调全局低维预测几何的涌现，这一图景并不完整。在带聚类的回归问题中，多层感知机（MLP）会自然形成单义化（monosemantic）的专用神经元：单个神经元与特定预测特征强对齐，该特征只对输入空间的某一局部区域起作用。MLP并非学习单一的全局低维表示，而是学习一组局部低维表示，这些局部表示合起来可以张成高维空间。这种专门化被证明能给MLP带来相对于基于全局低维表示的特征学习方法更高的数据效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征学习理论主要关注神经网络如何涌现出全局低维预测几何，作者认为这种描述不完整，无法解释神经网络在实际数据（尤其是聚类数据）中的行为与优势，因此需要更细致的理论刻画。

Method: 针对带聚类的回归问题，作者分析MLP的特征组织方式，通过展示单个神经元与特定局部预测特征的强对齐来揭示单义化专用神经元的涌现；并进一步证明这种局部低维表示集合（可共同张成高维空间）所构成的表示结构，并以理论方式对比其与全局低维表示方法在数据效率上的差异。

Result: 证明MLP学习的是一组局部低维表示而非单个全局低维表示，并严格证明这种局部专门化结构使MLP在数据效率上优于基于全局低维表示的特征学习方法。

Conclusion: 神经网络的表示学习机制需要从“单一全局几何”扩展为“多个局部低维表示的集合”这一更完整的视角；局部单义化与专门化为MLP带来可证明的数据效率优势。

Abstract: Understanding how neural networks learn and organize features is central to understanding their behavior. Much existing theory of feature learning has focused on the emergence of a global low-dimensional predictive geometry. We show that this picture is incomplete. In regression problems with clustered data, we demonstrate that multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) naturally develop monosemantic specialized neurons: individual neurons become strongly aligned with a specific predictive feature relevant to a particular region of the input space. Rather than learning a single global low-dimensional representation, MLPs learn a collection of local low-dimensional representations that can collectively span a high-dimensional space. This specialization provably gives MLPs a data-efficiency advantage over feature-learning methods based on a global low-dimensional representation.

</details>


### [162] [ChorusTIC: Training-Free Multivariate Time Series Classification via Chorus In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24033)
*Juntao Fang,Shifeng Xie,Ruichu Cai,Shengji Zheng,Zijian Li,Keli Zhang,Lujia Pan,Themis Palpanas,Zhifeng Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChorusTIC是一个面向时间序列分类的原生基础模型，通过上下文内学习（in-context learning）直接对任意通道配置的多变量时间序列进行分类，无需对目标任务进行参数更新或训练任务专用分类器。它仅在合成标记片段上预训练，在UEA-30和UCR-128全部基准上实现了强全上下文与低标注性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要支持预测和可迁移的表示学习，而分类任务仍需在目标数据集上拟合任务专用分类器；同时多变量输入的各个通道常被独立编码，无法有效建模跨通道交互。作者希望构建一个"分类原生"的基础模型，能够在异构通道配置下进行上下文内分类，免除目标任务的参数更新。

Method: ChorusTIC包含三个核心组件：1) 片段一致随机子通道槽拼接（Episode-consistent Random Subchannel Slot Concatenation），将可变的通道配置映射到与原始通道数无关的固定宽度表示；2) 共享双轴编码器（dual-axis encoder），同时建模时间维度与跨通道交互；3) 特征轴校准（利用上下文导出的分布）与防泄漏的上下文内学习来预测查询标签。预训练仅使用合成标记片段（包含共享任务背景的上下文集与查询集），类别由稀疏的时间或跨通道规则区分。

Result: 在完整UEA-30和UCR-128档案上的评估显示，ChorusTIC在无需目标任务专用分类器拟合的情况下，取得了强劲的全上下文（full-context）和低标注（low-label）性能，验证了跨异构通道配置的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ChorusTIC证明了单靠合成数据训练的分类原生基础模型，可以通过上下文内学习在不同通道配置间泛化，无需目标侧参数更新，为时间序列分类提供了一个有前景的新范式。

Abstract: Time series classification underpins applications in healthcare, sensing, and industrial monitoring. Although time series foundation models support forecasting and transferable representation learning, classification still typically requires fitting a task-specific classifier on each target dataset, while individual channels of multivariate inputs are often encoded independently. We introduce ChorusTIC, a classification-native foundation model for in-context classification across heterogeneous channel configurations without target-task parameter updates. ChorusTIC combines episode-consistent Random Subchannel Slot Concatenation with a shared dual-axis encoder to model temporal and cross-channel interactions and map variable channel configurations into a fixed-width representation independent of the original channel count. It then calibrates feature axes using context-derived distributions and predicts query labels through leakage-protected in-context learning. We pretrain ChorusTIC solely on synthetic labeled episodes comprising context and query sets that share a task background, with classes distinguished by sparse temporal or cross-channel rules. Evaluations on the complete UEA-30 and UCR-128 archives show strong full-context and low-label performance without target-specific classifier fitting.

</details>


### [163] [PinSieve: Production Selective VLM Serving and a Governed Memory Flywheel for Enterprise Content-Quality Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24040)
*Chuqing Gao,Yuanfang Song,Jonathan Zhang,Yifan Wu,Vishwakarma Singh,Qinglong Zeng,Andrey Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PinSieve是在大规模内容质量管道中的生产案例研究。其核心部署组件是一个"选择性"VLM服务代理（Serving Agent），仅处理轻量级上游模型无法解决的灰区（grey-zone）样本，在线暴露标量路由分数，并保留受控的人工升级机制。在灰区切片上，该系统过滤的非可操作项数量是旧生产模块的2.05倍，同时略微降低估计漏检率；推广后审阅生产力提升25.7%，归一化运营成本降低16.2%，信号交付由次日转为当日。论文进一步研究受治理的记忆飞轮（memory flywheel）维护机制，其中被升级项默认审阅、自动通过项主要通过审计抽样打标签；六个月的链式月度刷新将平均FNR@50%从17.73%降至13.29%。该服务代理配方已迁移至多个内部信号，表明具有跨任务可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业生产环境中的AI代理往往需要是有界（bounded）、有状态（stateful）、可观测（observable）和可治理（governable）的，而非完全自主。作者关注在内容质量管道这类生产任务中，如何设计一个既能充分利用VLM能力、又不越权/失控的选择性代理系统，并研究其在选择性反馈下如何持续维护和演进。

Method: （1）部署层：选择性VLM Serving Agent，仅在轻量级上游模型未解决的灰区切片上运行，在线暴露标量路由分数，并保留受控的人工升级（escalation）路径。（2）治理层：受治理的记忆飞轮（governed memory flywheel），在选择性反馈（升级项默认审阅、自动通过项以审计抽样打标签）下工作。Feedback Memory记录路由轨迹、观察路径、审计倾向与回放元数据，用于评估和调试。（3）数据策展代理（Data Curation Agent）采用有界的"提案-验证"循环，综合代表性、不确定性、时效性和新鲜审阅回放，并在批量接受前加入正率（positive-rate）和分数桶（score-bin）护栏。（4）推理审阅代理（Reasoning Review Agent）审计教师生成的推理（rationale），支持保留/修复/丢弃决策。所有生产声明仅归于已部署的Serving Agent；回放与推理审阅结果视为离线或抽样治理证据。

Result: 在灰区切片上，部署系统过滤的非可操作项数量为旧生产模块的2.05倍，且估计漏检率略降；推广后审阅生产力提升25.7%，归一化运营成本降低16.2%，信号交付从次日变为当日。在六个月生产数据的链式月度刷新中，该设计将平均FNR@50%从代表性随机回放下的17.73%降至13.29%。同一服务代理配方已被多个内部信号采用，显示跨任务可转移性。

Conclusion: 有界、可观测、可治理的选择性代理设计在生产环境中优于完全自治方案：通过灰区路由、在线分数暴露和受控升级来确保可控性，通过结构化记忆飞轮与回放守门机制实现受治理的数据维护，既显著提升效率、降低成本、加速交付，又具备可迁移的通用配方。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents in production often need to be bounded, stateful, observable, and governable rather than fully autonomous. We present PinSieve, a production case study in a large-scale content-quality pipeline. Its deployed component is a selective vision-language-model (VLM) Serving Agent that operates only on the grey-zone slice left unresolved by lightweight upstream models, exposes a scalar routing score online, and preserves controlled human escalation. On this slice, the deployed system filters 2.05x more non-actionable items than the previous production module while slightly reducing estimated miss rate; after promotion, it improves review productivity by 25.7%, reduces normalized operating cost by 16.2%, and moves signal delivery from next-day to same-day. We then study maintenance through a governed memory flywheel under selective feedback, where escalated items are reviewed by default and auto-passed items are labeled mainly through audit sampling. Feedback Memory records routing traces, observation paths, audit propensities, and replay metadata for evaluation and debugging. The Data Curation Agent uses a bounded proposal-verifier loop over representative, uncertainty, recency, and fresh-review replay, with positive-rate and score-bin guardrails before batch acceptance. In chained monthly refresh over six months of production data, this design reduces average FNR@50% from 17.73% under representative random replay to 13.29%. A Reasoning Review Agent audits teacher-generated rationales and supports keep/repair/drop decisions. Production claims are attributed only to the deployed Serving Agent; replay and rationale-review results are offline or sampled-governance evidence. The same serving-agent recipe has been adopted to several additional internal signals, suggesting transferability beyond one task.

</details>


### [164] [XP-JEPA: Cross-Predictive Physics Grounding for Forecastable Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24044)
*Kehan Wen,Ziming Li,Siyuan Luo,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 XP-JEPA（Cross-Predictive Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture），将视觉潜在动力学以特权物理轨迹为锚定进行训练：视觉与物理两个模态分别编码，由共享的动作条件预测器推进，并要求预测同时匹配两模态的未来表示。训练后丢弃物理分支，部署时仅保留纯视觉模型。实验表明该方法显著降低新拟合预测器的 rollout 漂移（0.361→0.104），并将平均控制成功率从 53.6% 提升至 78.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自预测模型（self-predictive models）中，编码器与预测器联合优化时会发生协同适应（co-adaptation），使得潜在状态转变容易预测但仅微弱受场景物理演化约束，导致潜在动力学缺乏物理根基，制约基于 rollouts 的控制。

Method: XP-JEPA 对视觉观测和物理状态分别编码，二者经同一条动作条件预测器向前传播，每个预测同时匹配两模态的未来表示，从而鼓励跨模态统一的潜在动力学并以真实物理转移为锚定。训练结束后丢弃物理分支，测试时仅使用视觉模型；与直接做物理状态回归（physical-state regression）的基线进行对比。

Result: 在包含六个评估子族的多任务套件上，XP-JEPA 将新拟合预测器的 rollout 漂移降至 0.104（基线 0.361），平均控制成功率升至 78.2%（基线 53.6%）；而直接物理状态回归虽提升位置可解码性，但预测性与控制与纯视觉基线基本持平。

Conclusion: 交叉预测的物理锚定（cross-predictive physical grounding）能产生更具可预测性的潜在动力学，从而改善基于 rollout 的控制，且测试时无需特权物理输入。

Abstract: Latent world models plan by predicting how candidate actions transform learned representations. In self-predictive models, however, the encoder and predictor are optimized jointly and can co-adapt to latent transitions that are easy to predict but only weakly constrained by the physical evolution of the scene. We introduce the cross-predictive JEPA (XP-JEPA), which grounds visual latent dynamics in privileged physical trajectories. XP-JEPA separately encodes visual observations and physical states, advances both through a shared action-conditioned predictor, and matches each prediction to both future representations. This objective encourages unified latent dynamics across the two modalities, grounded in the underlying physical transitions. The physical branch is discarded after training, leaving a visual-only model at deployment. On a multi-task suite spanning six evaluation subfamilies, XP-JEPA reduces rollout drift of a newly fitted predictor from $0.361$ to $0.104$ and increases mean control success from $53.6\%$ to $78.2\%$. Direct physical-state regression raises position decodability but leaves forecastability and control near the visual-only baseline. Cross-predictive physical grounding can therefore produce more forecastable latent dynamics for rollout-based control without privileged inputs at test time.

</details>


### [165] [Physics-Integrated Operator Learning via Gaussian Splatting Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24049)
*Jihao Zhang,Junyi Guo,Jian-Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种表示层面的物理融合方法：利用前馈高斯溅射（FFGS）连续表示作为离散解场与偏微分方程（PDE）算子之间的接口，通过自带闭式空间导数的高斯场将已知物理算子直接嵌入学习到的演化映射中，无需物理残差损失。在2D/3D多个PDE系统（平流、扩散、非线性自平流、反应动力学）的长时程自回归预测中，相对ℓ2误差较最强纯数据驱动基线降低1.5×~2.2×，并持续提升谱保真度；在方程部分已知时仍保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子为时空PDE系统提供高效代理模型，但纯数据驱动方法在长时程自回归预测中会累积误差，且未能利用已有的控制方程结构；现有物理融合方法多采用残差训练目标或PDE特定架构约束，前者带来优化困难，后者限制架构通用性。

Method: 提出表示层面的物理集成框架：用前馈高斯溅射（FFGS）将离散解状态重建为连续高斯场，该表示具有闭式空间导数，因此可直接在学习的演化映射内部融合已知物理PDE算子，而无需引入物理残差损失。

Result: 在2D与3D PDE基准（平流、扩散、非线性自平流、反应动力学）上的长时程自回归推演中，相对ℓ2误差相较最强纯数据驱动基线降低1.5×~2.2×，并一致提升频谱保真度；在控制方程部分已知时依然有效，展现对不完整物理的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 连续场表示可作为将已知物理结构融入通用神经算子代理模型的实际接口，兼具准确性与对不完整物理信息的鲁棒性，为表示级物理融合提供了一条通用路径。

Abstract: Neural operators provide efficient surrogates for spatiotemporal PDE systems, but purely data-driven formulations often accumulate substantial errors during long-horizon autoregressive prediction and may fail to exploit available governing-equation structure. Existing approaches incorporate physics primarily through residual-based training objectives or PDE-specific architectural constraints, which can introduce optimization difficulties or limit architectural generality. In this work, we introduce a representation-level approach to physics integration in which a feed-forward Gaussian splatting (FFGS) representation serves as a continuous interface between discretized solution fields and governing operators. The FFGS representation reconstructs the state as a continuous Gaussian field with closed-form spatial derivatives, allowing available physical PDE operators to be integrated directly within the learned evolution map without introducing a physics-residual loss. We evaluate the framework across two- and three-dimensional PDE systems, including advection, diffusion, nonlinear self-advection, and reaction dynamics. Over long-horizon autoregressive rollouts, the proposed framework reduces relative $\ell_2$ error by $1.5\times$--$2.2\times$ compared with the strongest purely data-driven baseline across the benchmark suite, while consistently improving spectral fidelity. The framework also remains effective when the governing equations are partially known, demonstrating robustness to incomplete physics. These results demonstrate that continuous field representations can provide a practical interface for incorporating known physical structure into generic neural-operator surrogates.

</details>


### [166] [ALPHABET: A Laplace-Pole History Aggregator with Banked Exponential Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24051)
*Daehwa Ko,JaeHyeon Kim,Oh Seong Kwon,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALPHABET是一个仅用数千参数就能与大型基线竞争的紧凑线性时间序列模型，它通过将时间历史压缩为稳定的复极点模态、以“可显式审计”的模态能量与滞后矩作为预测特征，在82个任务的基准上取得平均排名3.97，并在D=64锚点下比九个基线平均快5.02倍（推理）和3.93倍（训练）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探究一个根本问题：序列模型能否在只有几千个参数、且预测接口完全可审计的条件下，仍然保持与传统大模型相当的竞争力。这一动机源于对模型规模、可解释性和计算效率之间平衡的追求。

Method: ALPHABET采用线性时间架构。其核心机制是将时间历史压缩为稳定的复极点模态：一个直接滤波器组（direct bank）将模态状态综合回特征轨迹；另一个独立的级联滤波器组（cascaded bank）对该变换后的轨迹进行分析而不做重综合；最终由一个仿射头（affine head）仅读取来自两个滤波器的模态能量与滞后矩。作者还给出了该描述符的时序信息刻画：对于平稳、完全观测的特征过程，每个模态能量是对二阶谱的频域局部化测量，此类测量的连续统足以识别整个谱，且几乎每个模态都能分离任意给定的有限个谱上可区分的类别。

Result: 在具有匹配低滞后统计量的高斯控制任务中，当原始自协方差仍停留在随机水平时，学习到的描述符已接近贝叶斯最优（Bayes oracle）。在固定的82任务注册表上，ALPHABET在完整的十族比较中取得平均排名3.97。在常用宽度D=64的运行锚点下，其6,437个参数相对九个基线实现了平均5.02倍的推理加速和3.93倍的完整训练步数加速。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：一个极小的、带有明确可审计预测接口的序列模型完全可以在任务精度上与九个大型基线家族竞争，同时以数量级更低的成本和更强的可解释性取胜，表明“小模型+谱模态描述符”是一种富有前景的序列建模范式。

Abstract: Can a sequence model remain competitive with only a few thousand parameters and an explicitly auditable prediction interface? We introduce ALPHABET, a compact linear-time model that compresses temporal history into stable complex pole modes: a direct bank synthesizes its modal states back into the feature trajectory, an independent cascaded bank analyzes the transformed trajectory without resynthesis, and an affine head reads only modal energies and lag moments from both banks. We characterize the temporal information this descriptor retains: for a stationary, fully observed feature process, each mode energy is a frequency-localized measurement of the second-order spectrum, the continuum of such measurements identifies the spectrum, and almost every mode separates any fixed finite set of spectrally distinct classes. On a Gaussian control with matched low-lag statistics, the learned descriptor approaches the Bayes oracle where raw autocovariances remain at chance. Across the fixed 82-task registry, ALPHABET attains mean rank 3.97 in the complete ten-family comparison. At the common-width D=64 runtime anchor, its 6,437 parameters deliver 5.02 times faster inference and 3.93 times faster complete training steps than the nine baselines on average.

</details>


### [167] [PhysicsBench: A Unified Leaderboard for Generative and Predictive Models in Engineering Design and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24056)
*Sang Won Lee,Hyogu Jeong,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysicsBench，一个统一标准化流程的基准与排行榜，用于评估几何生成和物理场/标量预测的AI模型。它涵盖1D/2D/3D七个任务、九大数据集（含工业级CAD/CFD/FEA模拟与公开基准）、28种配置，统一对66个模型进行排名。评估采用从S到XL的有限数据规模，而非学术基准常见的无限训练集；并通过BenchRank（基于PageRank的支配图与指标去偏）将质量指标与计算成本分列排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式与预测式AI模型在工程设计与仿真中被广泛应用，但目前这些模型往往被孤立评估：使用学术数据集、不受约束的规模、不一致的指标与流程，导致“state-of-the-art”多为自我报告式声明，缺乏统一的、可复现的模型选择依据。

Method: 构建涵盖1D/2D/3D七个生成与预测任务的统一基准；在九个数据集（工业级CAD/CFD/FEA仿真及公开参考）上扩展为28种配置，采用S到XL的有限数据规模；以统一指标套件衡量几何保真度（分布距离）、物理场与标量精度，以及工程特定的场/形状有效性；提出BenchRank，通过去偏（debiasing）相关指标并基于头对头支配图上的PageRank进行排序，同时将计算成本置于独立的效率视图。

Result: 实验表明：模型的学术大规模排名对其小数据排名仅有弱预测力；在七个任务中有六个任务的最优模型随数据规模变化而变化；没有任何一个模型能主导超过一个任务。BenchRank将“state-of-the-art”从自我报告声明转为公开、可发布的模型选择基础。

Conclusion: PhysicsBench提供了一个覆盖数据规模、任务类型和指标体系的统一评估框架，揭示现有模型在真实工业规模下表现与学术排名不一致，从而为工程领域的模型选择建立了公开且标准化的基础。

Abstract: Generative and predictive artificial intelligence models are increasingly used to generate geometry and to predict physical fields and scalar quantities in engineering design and simulation. Yet these models are typically evaluated in isolation, on academic datasets at unconstrained scales, with inconsistent metrics and procedures. We present PhysicsBench, a unified benchmark and leaderboard that evaluates generative and predictive models under one standardized procedure. PhysicsBench spans seven generation and prediction tasks across 1D, 2D, and 3D domains and ranks 66 models on nine datasets, comprising industrial-scale CAD/CFD/FEA simulations and public references, expanded into 28 configurations. One procedure and ranking apply to both families, each ranked within its own tasks. Evaluation spans realistic, limited data scales from S to XL rather than the unlimited training sets common in academic benchmarks. A common metric suite captures geometric fidelity with distributional distances, physical-field and scalar accuracy, and engineering-specific field- and shape-validity. BenchRank debiases correlated metrics and ranks by PageRank over a head-to-head dominance graph, so every reported quality metric is also ranked, with computational cost in a separate efficiency view. Across tasks, an architecture's large-scale academic standing weakly predicts its small-data ranking. The top model changes with data scale in six of the seven tasks, and no model leads more than one task. PhysicsBench turns "state-of-the-art" from a self-reported claim into an openly published foundation for model selection.

</details>


### [168] [Mechanistic Circuit Identification for Controllable Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24065)
*Nakyung Lee,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于电路（circuit）的可解释数据生成框架，将训练动力学驱动的数据估值与机制可解释性（MI）相结合。作者发现模型内部存在因果控制"可学习性、挑战性、一致性"三类数据效用的专用电路，并以这些电路作为可控接口替代启发式提示来控制数据生成；进一步提出 SAMS（阶段感知机制调度），根据模型动态优化需求安排电路引导的数据。在多项选择题问答任务上，该方法生成的数据更具多样性，持续提升下游性能与校准效果，开创了将 MI 用作实用可控接口的白盒数据生成范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据合成管线大多依赖基于启发式提示的黑盒控制，无法洞察单个样本如何与模型底层学习动力学相互作用。作者希望弥合训练动力学数据估值与机制可解释性之间的鸿沟，建立一种白盒、可解释的数据生成范式，从而更精确地按模型需求制造数据。

Method: 1) 将数据质量概念化为三条互补效用轴：可学习性（learnability）、挑战性（challenge）和一致性（alignment）。2) 定位模型内部专门因果控制这些效用信号的电路。3) 将电路作为可控界面，主动引导生成面向特定效用的数据，取代启发式提示。4) 提出 SAMS（Stage-Aware Mechanistic Scheduling），依据模型不断变化的优化需求调度电路引导的数据。

Result: 在多项选择题问答任务上的实验表明，该方法能够生成精确控制且比提示基线更多样化的数据，并持续改善下游任务性能与校准效果。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了可解释数据生成的白盒范式，证明了机制可解释性不仅可以作为分析工具，更可以作为实用、可控的数据生成界面，为连接模型内部机制与数据工程提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: While recent advances in data synthesis aim to curate high-quality datasets, most generation pipelines still rely on heuristic prompt-based control. This black-box paradigm provides limited insight into how individual samples interact with a model's underlying learning dynamics. To bridge this gap, we propose a circuit-grounded framework that connects training-dynamics-based data valuation with mechanistic interpretability (MI). Specifically, we conceptualize data quality along three complementary utility axes, learnability, challenge, and alignment. First, we uncover specialized model-internal circuits that causally govern these utility signals. Then, moving beyond heuristic prompting toward mechanistic control, we leverage these circuits as controllable interfaces, actively steering generation to produce utility-targeted data. Building on this capability, we introduce SAMS (Stage-Aware Mechanistic Scheduling), which schedules circuit-steered data according to the model's evolving optimization needs. Experiments on multiple-choice QA tasks demonstrate that our approach yields precisely controlled data with greater diversity than prompt-based baselines, consistently improving downstream performance and calibration. Ultimately, this work establishes a principled white-box paradigm for interpretable data generation, pioneering the use of MI not just as an analytical tool, but as a practical, controllable interface.

</details>


### [169] [A Feature-Major Codebook for Memory-Efficient Sparse-Binary Self-Organizing Maps: Scaling a MEDLINE Atlas to 1.05 Million Neurons on a Single Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24067)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅改变自组织映射(SOM)码本存储布局的方法（按特征主序存储，使权重列在样本块上复用），在不改变实现、精度与更新规则的前提下，将BMU搜索重构为分块稀疏-稠密乘积，使MEDLINE规模（2990万篇）的SOM训练在单张24GB GPU上约72秒收敛，并扩展到104.8万神经元——据作者所知是迄今最大规模的SOM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建MEDLINE规模的SOM图集一直不切实际：主导训练的BMU（最佳匹配单元）搜索每轮迭代都需按带宽读取整个码本，成为性能瓶颈。作者认为该瓶颈在很大程度上是码本布局造成的伪影，而非本质算力限制，因此希望仅通过数据布局优化来消除该瓶颈。

Method: 将码本按特征主序（feature-major）存储，使每个特征的权重连续存放，形式为W[v.M+i]；这把BMU搜索转化为分块稀疏-稠密乘积，使每次加载的权重列可被一整块样本复用，从而提升内存带宽利用率。除布局外保持实现、精度与更新规则完全一致；配套采用与半径无关的box-blur更新和基于收敛的停止规则。

Result: 仅改变布局即带来4.5–8.5倍的BMU搜索加速；由于精确argmin BMU对存储方式不变，该加速无代价——留出量化误差与cuSPARSE基线相差在0.5%以内。相对基线呈现交叉而非恒定优势：小图时cuSPARSE.SOM更快，128x128时SparseBin.SOM快1.5倍、256x256快2.6倍，512x512时是24GB显存下唯一可运行的方案；在141GB H200上达到104.8万神经元（1024x1024）。留出误差在三个量级的图规模上呈平滑幂律且无拐点，表明分辨率上限由算力而非数据决定。在相当工作量下比MedSOM快约82倍，比最佳多核CPU库在128x128下快621倍。

Conclusion: 码本布局是SOM大规模训练的决定性因素，仅通过feature-major存储即可将MEDLINE规模SOM训练从不可行变为秒级可行，并支持远超先前报道的映射规模；误差随规模呈平滑幂律表明其扩展性由计算资源主导，为构建大规模可浏览文献图集提供了实用路径。

Abstract: A self-organising map turns a large corpus into a browsable two-dimensional atlas, but building one at MEDLINE scale has been impractical: the best-matching-unit (BMU) search that dominates training is bound by the bandwidth needed to read the codebook every epoch. I show that this bottleneck is largely an artefact of codebook layout. Storing it feature-major with each feature's weights contiguous, W[v.M+i], recasts the search as a tiled sparse-dense product in which every loaded weight column is reused across a tile of samples. Varying only the layout, with implementation, precision and update rule held fixed, accelerates the BMU search by 4.5-8.5x. Because an exact-argmin BMU is invariant to how the codebook is stored, this gain costs nothing: held-out quantisation error agrees with a cuSPARSE baseline to within 0.5% at every map size. Against that baseline the advantage is a crossover rather than a constant: cuSPARSE.SOM is faster at small maps, SparseBin.SOM is 1.5x faster at 128x128 and 2.6x at 256x256, and at 512x512 it is the only one that runs at all on 24 GB. Paired with a radius-independent box-blur update and a convergence-based stopping rule, it trains a converged map over 29.9 million MEDLINE articles in about 72 s at 64x64 on one 24 GB GPU, and accommodates 262,144 neurons (512x512 edges) where every alternative algorithm I tested exceeds memory constraints. On a 141 GB H200 it reaches 1,048,576 neurons (1024x1024 edges) - to my knowledge the largest self-organising map yet reported. Held-out error follows a smooth power law with no elbow across three decades of map size, so the limit on resolution is compute rather than any breakpoint in the data. At matched work the design is ~82x faster than MedSOM, the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases and, at 128x128, 621x faster than the best available multicore-CPU library.

</details>


### [170] [Knowing When to Ask for Help: Bayesian Self-Escalation in Hierarchical LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24087)
*Nadeem Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"推理中途委派"范式：LLM智能体在自身推理过程中识别出成功概率低时，动态将控制权转移给更强的模型。作者将其建模为基于学习得到的能力后验分布的贝叶斯最优停时问题，推导出近视升级阈值闭合解、最优策略为时变阈值，并证明了指数分离、遗憾界与1/sqrt(n)有限样本保证，通过仿真和Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B→7B代码级联验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统的委派要么发生在推理开始前（路由器选模型），要么在完整响应完成后（验证器打分并可能重试）。本文研究第三种机制：智能体在其自身推理过程中识别出自己不太可能成功，并将控制权移交给更强模型，以弥补前两类的时机缺陷。

Method: 将推理中委派形式化为基于学习的能力后验分布的贝叶斯最优停时问题，其充分统计量从标注轨迹中学习而非直接读取原始熵。推导出近视升级阈值的闭合解；用动态规划刻画最优策略；证明最优策略是时变阈值且对原始信号无形状假设。进一步证明了oracle信念在Chernoff信息率下的指数分离、由后验校准控制的遗憾界，以及用n条标注校准轨迹时plug-in策略遗憾以1/sqrt(n)衰减的有限样本保证。

Result: 受控仿真研究确认了包括1/sqrt(n)速率在内的每一条理论预测。真实模型验证在Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B→7B代码级联（MBPP，257个任务）上确认了三个预注册预测中的两个：升级前沿在同等成本下优于事后路由，且累积能力信念的区分度随生成过程上升。

Conclusion: 推理过程中的"中途升级"在理论上可被最优地形式化为时变阈值的贝叶斯最优停时问题，具备严格的收敛与校准保证，并在真实模型级联上展现出优于事后路由的性能，同时指出第三个预注册预测未获确认。

Abstract: Current LLM agent systems decide delegation before reasoning begins (a router picks a model) or after a response is complete (a verifier scores it and may retry). We study a third regime: an agent that recognises, during its own reasoning, that it is unlikely to succeed and transfers control to a stronger model. We formulate intra-generation delegation as a Bayesian optimal-stopping problem over a learned competence posterior -- an online estimate of the agent's eventual task success whose sufficient statistics are learned from labelled trajectories, not read off raw entropy. We derive the myopic escalation threshold in closed form, characterise the optimal policy via dynamic programming, and prove that the optimal policy is a time-varying threshold with no shape assumption on the raw signal. We further prove exponential separation of the oracle belief at the Chernoff-information rate of the signal, a regret bound governed by the calibration of the posterior, and a finite-sample guarantee: with n labelled calibration trajectories the deployed plug-in policy's regret decays as 1/sqrt(n). A controlled simulation study confirms each prediction of the theory, including the predicted 1/sqrt(n) rate. We additionally report a real-model validation on a Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B->7B code cascade (MBPP, 257 tasks), confirming two of three pre-registered predictions: the escalation frontier dominates post-hoc routing at equal cost, and the cumulative competence belief's discrimination rises over generation.

</details>


### [171] [The Sharp Tail of Uniform Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24098)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个确定性、$\gamma$-均匀稳定的有界损失学习问题，证明其泛化差距尾部满足 $\mathbb P(R(A_S)-R_S(A_S)\ge c'\min\{L,\gamma p+L\sqrt{p/n}\})\ge e^{-p}$（对 $1\le p\le cn$ 同时成立），从而填补了已知构造仅适用于辅助弱相关随机变量、且下界仅在常数概率下成立的空白；与无对数的上界结合，精确刻画了均匀稳定性在高概率与矩意义下的最优依赖关系（精确到通用常数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀稳定性理论给出了泛化差距的高概率上界 $O(\gamma\log(1/\delta)+L\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)/n})$，但此前缺乏匹配的下界：已知构造只能针对点态范围随 $n$ 增长的辅助弱相关随机变量实现线性依赖，而已有学习下界仅对常数概率成立。作者旨在构造一个真实的有界损失学习问题，同时实现对所有置信水平匹配的下界，从而闭合这一缺口。

Method: 构造基于普通的有界绝对值损失回归（常数标签），核心是多重尺度（几何间隔排列）的罕见 Rademacher 特征集合：坐标方向上的 ramp 函数在超范数下保持均匀稳定性；奇对称化的最大值（odd symmetrized maximum）将唯一的极端特征转化为约 $\gamma p$ 的间隙，同时不破坏损失界；几何间隔的 ramps 将不同置信水平整合进同一个问题。

Result: 对任意 $n$、稳定性水平 $\gamma$ 和损失上界 $L$，构造了一个确定性 $\gamma$-均匀稳定问题，其尾部同时满足 $1\le p\le cn$ 时的下界 $\mathbb P\ge e^{-p}$。该构造与无对数上界联合，确定了大偏差与矩意义下均匀稳定性的最优依赖关系，精确到通用常数。

Conclusion: 该工作完全闭合了均匀稳定性在有限样本高概率泛化界上的上下界缺口，证明泛化差距的最优高概率与矩行为均被通用常数刻画，为均匀稳定性理论提供了紧的刻画。

Abstract: Uniform stability controls how much one training example can change the loss at any test point. A new logarithmic-free upper bound shows that a $γ$-uniformly stable algorithm with loss in $[0,L]$ has generalization gap at most $O \left(γ\log(1/δ) +L\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/δ)}{n}}\right)$ with probability $1-δ$. Whether an actual bounded-loss learning algorithm can realize the linear dependence on $\log(1/δ)$ has remained open. The known construction realizes it only for auxiliary weakly dependent random variables whose pointwise range grows with $n$. The known learning lower bound holds only at constant probability. We close this gap. For every $n$, stability level $γ$, and loss bound $L$, we construct one deterministic $γ$-uniformly stable learning problem whose tail satisfies, simultaneously for $1\le p\le c n$, $\mathbb P \left( R(A_S)-R_S(A_S) \ge c'\min \left\{L,γp+L\sqrt{p/n}\right\} \right)\ge e^{-p}.$ The construction is ordinary bounded absolute-loss regression with constant labels. Its key is a multiscale collection of rare Rademacher features. A coordinatewise ramp is stable in sup norm, while an odd symmetrized maximum converts a unique extreme feature into a gap of order $γp$ without violating the loss bound. Geometrically spaced ramps put all confidence levels into the same problem. Together with the logarithmic-free upper bound, this determines the optimal high-probability and moment dependence of uniform stability up to universal constants.

</details>


### [172] [Structured Frequency-Domain Evidence for LLM-Based Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24113)
*Jungwook Seo,Sangwon Son,Minjeong Kim,Seungmin Han,Seojin Yoo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向零样本LLM时间序列异常检测(TSAD)的证据增强框架，在保留索引化和去季节化时域观测的基础上，通过FFT补充全局与局部两分辨率的频域证据，从而显式暴露周期结构与谱偏移，提升多种多模态LLM在TSB-AD-U子集上的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常不仅表现为点状偏差，还可能表现为周期性结构的变化（如周期平移或局部振荡波动）。现有基于LLM的TSAD方法主要通过索引值、绘图或去季节化表示来提供时域证据，频谱结构仍隐式存在、未被充分利用，限制了LLM对周期性异常的识别能力。

Method: 构建证据增强的零样本TSAD框架：保留去季节化的索引化观测数据，同时利用FFT生成紧凑的频域证据，并分为两个分辨率——全局频域证据概括序列级周期上下文，局部频域证据捕捉时间局部谱偏离。

Result: 在AnomLLM框架上分别用InternVL2-LLaMA3-76B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct、Gemini-2.5-Flash和GPT-4o进行评估，并在TSB-AD-U子集上验证，结果表明显式频域证据显著提升了基于LLM的TSAD基线性能。

Conclusion: 频域证据能够有效补充索引化与去季节化的时域输入，为零样本LLM时间序列异常检测提供更全面的证据支持。

Abstract: Time-series anomalies can appear not only as pointwise deviations but also as changes in recurring temporal structure, such as shifted periodicity or localized oscillatory fluctuations. However, existing LLM-based time-series anomaly detection methods mainly expose time-domain evidence through indexed values, plots, or de-seasonalized representations, leaving spectral structure implicit. We propose an evidence-augmented zero-shot TSAD framework that preserves indexed de-seasonalized observations while adding compact frequency-domain evidence computed with the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT). The evidence is constructed at two resolutions: global frequency-domain evidence summarizes sequence-level periodic context, while local frequency-domain evidence captures time-localized spectral departures. Experiments on AnomLLM with InternVL2-LLaMA3-76B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and GPT-4o, together with evaluation on the TSB-AD-U subset, show that explicit frequency-domain evidence improves LLM-based TSAD baselines. These results suggest that frequency-domain evidence can complement indexed and de-seasonalized time-domain inputs for zero-shot LLM-based TSAD.

</details>


### [173] [A mesh-free multiresolution deep energy method with phase-field modeling of brittle fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24126)
*Han Zhang,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari,Babak Shahbodagh,Mohammad Vahab,Cosmin Anitescu,Timon Rabczuk,Elena Atroshchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单神经网络的无网格相场断裂求解方法：网络同时表示位移场和相场，通过多分辨率 C^1 二次 B 样条特征编码控制可表达的最细尺度，并用每次优化迭代重新采样的分层蒙特卡洛积分估计增量能量。该组合是裂纹扩展的关键，二者缺一则裂纹无法前进。由于表示全局 C^1，二阶与四阶断裂能量密度可在同一离散化上运行。在六个算例中，载荷-位移曲线与匹配正则化长度的交错有限元参考结果一致；在公开多裂纹基准上，20 次零样本运行可对 90% 裂纹的激活/休眠状态正确分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂模型避免显式追踪裂纹，但要求足够密的离散化以分辨由正则化长度决定宽度的局部化带，且该带的路径事先未知；传统网格方法在精度与自适应上代价高。作者旨在实现一种无需网格、无需缓慢训练即可表达最细尺度的神经网络离散化方案。

Method: 以单个神经网络同时表示位移场与相场，直接最小化增量能量函数进行训练。坐标通过基于 C^1 二次 B 样条网格的多分辨率特征编码输入网络，最细可表达尺度由编码层级直接设定。能量由分层蒙特卡洛积分在每步优化迭代重新抽样的积分点上估计。该编码-积分配对被证明至关重要，且全局 C^1 平滑性使二阶与四阶断裂能量密度可在同一离散化上计算。

Result: 六类算例（单边缺口拉伸/剪切、厚壁圆环单样条片等）的载荷-位移曲线在匹配正则化长度下与交错有限元参考一致：单边缺口试验峰值载荷误差约 1%，裂纹拓扑变化处误差在 8% 以内。在公开随机多裂纹基准数据集的 20 次零样本运行中，方法正确分类 90% 种子裂纹的激活/休眠状态，而数据集作者的深度 Ritz 基线失败。对照实验显示积分点固定或编码过粗均导致裂纹无法扩展。

Conclusion: 多分辨率 B 样条特征编码与每轮重采样的蒙特卡洛积分相互配合是神经网络相场断裂求解成功的关键；该无网格方案在精度上可与交错有限元匹敌，且能推广到未见过的多裂纹配置，为无需网格、无需预知裂纹路径的断裂模拟提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Phase-field modeling of brittle fracture removes the need to track cracks explicitly by recasting their evolution as the minimization of an energy functional. In return it requires a discretization dense enough to resolve a localization band whose width is set by a regularization length and whose path is not known in advance. We propose a mesh-free discretization in which a single neural network represents the displacement and phase fields and is trained by minimizing the incremental energy directly. The coordinates enter the network through a multiresolution feature encoding built from $C^1$ quadratic B-spline grids, so the finest scale the representation can express is set by choice rather than reached through slow training, and the energy is estimated by stratified Monte Carlo integration on points redrawn at every optimizer iteration. This pairing proves critical, since the crack fails to advance both when the integration points are held fixed and when the encoding is too coarse to represent the band, while each ingredient tolerates a wide range of settings once the other is in place. Because the representation is globally $C^1$, the second- and the fourth-order fracture energy densities run on the identical discretization. Across six problems, from single-edge-notched tension and shear to a thick-walled ring on a single spline patch, the computed load-displacement curves follow staggered finite element references at matched regularization length, with peak loads within about 1% on the single-edge-notched tests and within 8% where the crack pattern changes topology. On a public benchmark dataset of random multi-crack configurations the method classifies the active or dormant state of 90% of the seeded cracks in twenty zero-shot runs, where the deep Ritz baseline of the dataset authors fails.

</details>


### [174] [From Gradient-Boosted Trees to Deep Recommenders: Practical Lessons from Migrating a Production Customer Support Recommender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24132)
*Sonia Sharma,Jeyendran Balakrishnan,Shreya Rajpal,Swapnil Parekh,Nagaraj Janardhana,Andrew Mattarella-Micke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个生产环境中的对话式推荐系统，如何从梯度提升多分类模型（CatBoost）成功迁移到成对二分类深度推荐模型，以应对产品目录从静态SKU到动态捆绑折扣商品的转变。系统通过成对二分类建模、负采样、噪声注入、注意力池化处理长对话转写，并在各对话阶段与CatBoost对比，在对话初期达到持平、后期表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快节奏服务型业务的产品目录正从静态独立定价的SKU转向动态捆绑与折扣组合，这超出了传统树分类器的能力：它们假设标签空间固定且变化缓慢，难以整合表格数据与对话转写等多模态信号。同时该推荐系统对生态增长举措和下游功能（如实时动态话术推送）至关重要，因此保证线上推荐质量是不可妥协的约束。

Method: 将推荐重新表述为成对二分类预测，从而联合学习用户特征与物品特征；通过负采样和噪声注入增强学习到的表示；对长对话实时上下文采用基于转写分块的注意力池化，并与TF-IDF和句子嵌入基线对比；探索多种架构（双塔模型、DeepFM及其变体）和对比损失等损失函数。

Result: 在所有对话阶段上以CatBoost为基线进行评测，该方法在对话初期达到基线持平，在对话后期阶段表现优于基线。

Conclusion: 成对二分类深度推荐框架配合注意力池化能够有效处理动态标签空间和多模态对话信号，可安全地用于生产环境的实时对话推荐，并随对话展开逐步提升推荐质量。

Abstract: Product catalogs in fast-moving service businesses are shifting from static, independently priced SKUs toward dynamically bundled, discount-coupled offerings--a shift that strains the tree-based classifiers traditionally preferred for sparse and highly imbalanced data. These classifiers assume a fixed, slowly changing label space and struggle to incorporate multimodal signals such as tabular data and transcripts. We present the migration of a live, production conversational recommendation system from a gradient-boosted multiclass model to a pairwise-binary deep recommender. Because this system is critical to ecosystem growth initiatives and downstream features like dynamic pitching--surfacing the most relevant pitch text to a support agent in real time during a live customer conversation--maintaining live recommendation quality was a non-negotiable constraint. We detail the techniques that made this migration successful--reformulating recommendation as pairwise binary prediction to learn jointly from user and item features, and enhancing learned representations via negative sampling and noise injection. To efficiently incorporate long, live conversation context, we apply attention pooling over transcript chunks and benchmark it against TF-IDF and sentence-embedding baselines. Finally, we explore multiple architectures (including two-tower models, DeepFM, and their variants) and loss functions such as contrastive loss. Evaluating against a CatBoost baseline across all conversational stages, we demonstrate that our approach achieves parity at conversation beginning and outperforms at later conversational stages.

</details>


### [175] [Steering Recurrent Reasoners at Inference Time with Readout Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24136)
*Shunsuke Kamiya,Masanori Koyama,Seongcheol Jeong,Fumiya Uchiyama,Kenji Kubo,Kohei Hayashi,Masahiro Suzuki,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重训练即可提升循环推理模型性能的测试时干预方法——Readout Feedback (RoFB)。该方法将模型自身的中间预测（readout）转换为逐 token 的两两耦合作用力，注入到潜在状态动力学中，从而在不增加推理步数或采样轨迹的前提下实现对潜在动力学的闭环引导。在 Sudoku 和 Maze 任务上对 AKOrN、ItrSA++、TRM 三种循环模型进行实验，六个模型-任务组合中有四个取得明显提升，且效果优于单纯增加步数或多轨迹采样，计算开销相当或更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型（RNN、深度循环推理模型等）通过共享计算块反复更新潜在状态，已成为解决复杂推理任务的强力架构。现有测试时扩展计算的方法要么增加推理步数，要么采更多轨迹，但都忽略了每条轨迹内部已揭示的信息。作者希望利用轨迹内部的信息（中间预测概率）来进一步改进推理，而无需重新训练。

Method: RoFB 是一种测试时干预机制：在推理过程中读取模型的中间输出（readout 概率），将其转换为逐 token 的成对耦合作用力（token-wise pairwise coupling forces），并注入到潜在状态的动力学更新中。这相当于把模型自身的输出作为反馈信号，形成闭环控制回路，对潜在状态演化进行引导（steering），从而在不调整模型参数、不增加推理步数的前提下提升求解能力。方法不依赖特定模型架构，可应用于不同的循环推理模型。

Result: 在 Sudoku 与 Maze 两个任务上，对 AKOrN、ItrSA++、TRM 三个循环模型进行评估，RoFB 在六个模型-任务组合中有四个取得了明显增益，达到仅靠增加推理步数或多轨迹采样无法企及的性能，且计算代价相当或更低。其余两个组合也保持与基线相当的表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对潜在动力学的闭环引导可以作为一种与现有推理时缩放方法互补的控制机制，为循环推理模型提供额外的性能提升途径。同时该思路具有可迁移性（跨模型、跨任务），暗示"利用自身输出反哺内部动态"的测试时控制范式值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Recurrent models, which repeatedly update latent states with shared computation blocks, have emerged as powerful architectures for solving complex reasoning tasks. Existing inference-time methods scale computation by running more steps or sampling more trajectories, but ignore information revealed within each trajectory. Here we show that recurrent models can be improved at inference time by using their own readout probabilities to steer latent dynamics without retraining. We introduce Readout Feedback (RoFB), a test-time intervention that converts intermediate predictions into token-wise pairwise coupling forces injected into the latent dynamics. Across three recurrent models (AKOrN, ItrSA++, TRM) on Sudoku and Maze, RoFB yields clear gains in four of six model-task pairs, achieving performance unattainable by merely running more steps or selecting from multiple trajectories, at comparable or lower computational cost. These results suggest that closed-loop steering of latent dynamics can serve as a complementary inference-time control mechanism for recurrent reasoning models.

</details>


### [176] [Robust Data-Collection Policy Learning for Low-Variance Online Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24146)
*Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Licheng Luo,Rohan Chandra,Nan Jiang,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双循环梯度算法，用于学习在转移函数存在不确定性时仍能高效且稳健地降低在线策略评估方差的行为策略，并给出了理论上的全局收敛保证和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的on-policy策略评估方法常因高方差而低效；现有的行为策略搜索可以降低在线评估方差，但假设转移函数已知，未考虑仿真环境与真实环境之间的转移模型误差，导致在真实环境中部署时方差仍然较高、样本成本昂贵。

Method: 提出一个双层（双循环）基于梯度的优化算法，联合优化行为策略使其在降低策略评估方差的同时对转移函数不确定性具有鲁棒性；推导了新颖的转移方差梯度表达式。

Result: 理论方面建立了算法的全局收敛保证；数值实验表明该方法对转移扰动比现有方法更不敏感，验证了其实际效用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将转移不确定性纳入行为策略搜索可以显著提升策略评估的稳健性与样本效率，为仿真到真实场景下的评估方差控制提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning policy evaluation, classic on-policy methods often suffer from high variance when estimating policy performance. To mitigate this issue, behavior policy search has been proposed to learn data-collecting policies tailored to reduce online evaluation variance. However, these approaches do not account for uncertainties in the transition functions. In practice, simulator transitions often differ from the real world due to modeling errors or approximation limitations. As a result, behavior policies trained in simulation may still yield high variance when deployed in real environments, leading to costly reliance on real-world evaluation samples. In this work, we propose a double-loop gradient-based algorithm for learning behavior policies that are both efficient and robust to transition uncertainty. Theoretically, we derive novel transition-variance gradient expressions and establish global convergence guarantees for the algorithm. Numerically, we demonstrate that our method is less sensitive to transition perturbations than existing approaches, providing supportive evidence for its practical utility.

</details>


### [177] [From Relaxed Indexability to Exact Indexability: A $t$-Step Approach for Partially Observable Restless Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24167)
*Qizhen Jia,Keqin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对部分可观测的 restless multi-armed bandit（RMAB），本文提出一种 t 步前瞻阈值策略来近似 Whittle index：阈值由 t 步有限时域价值迭代下的“主动减被动优势”定义，t=1 退化为 Liu[10] 的线性阈值，t>1 则随补贴 m 变化并更贴近真实决策边界。算法无需预先假定 indexability，并自带可索引性验证；理论上证明了在原始 Whittle 可索引性下近似指数 O(β^t) 收敛到精确 Whittle index，数值实验在 2715 个三状态实例上验证了可索引性并显著降低指数误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whittle index 策略是解决 restless multi-armed bandit 的可扩展方法，但在部分可观测下，即使在单个信念点求无差别补贴也需求解无闭式价值函数的无限时域信念状态问题。Liu[10] 通过线性化未知决策边界得到闭式近似 Whittle index，但其阈值仅基于一步主动-被动比较，未考虑更长时域的延续价值，精度受限。

Method: 将 Liu 的框架推广为 t 步前瞻阈值策略：对每个补贴 m，阈值由 t 步有限时域价值迭代下的主动减被动优势定义（即 active-minus-passive advantage）。t=1 时阈值与 m 无关并恢复 Liu 的线性阈值；t>1 时通过诱导的首穿（first-crossing）结构使阈值依赖补贴并更逼近精确决策边界。算法不要求 indexability 作为输入，并包含可索引性验证步骤。

Result: 在原始 Whittle 可索引性下证明 t 步近似 Whittle index 几何收敛：|Ŵ_t(ω)−W(ω)|=O(β^t)。数值上全部 2715 个三状态实例按所提准则验证为可索引；P95 指数误差从 t=1 的 2.18×10⁻² 降至 t=8 的 8.93×10⁻⁴；在 β=0.9999 的精确可比实例上 t=2 已恢复精确 Whittle 指数排序。中等深度阈值策略优于一步基线，接近最优动态规划基准，且运行时间随 t 仅温和增长。

Conclusion: t 步前瞻阈值通过引入更长时域的延续价值信息，在保持闭式/近闭式高效计算的同时显著提升部分可观测 RMAB 中 Whittle index 的近似精度，且具备可证明的指数收敛性、无需先验可索引性假设，是兼顾理论保证与实用数值性能的改进方案。

Abstract: Whittle index policies offer a scalable method for restless multi-armed bandits, but under partial observability even determining the indifference subsidy at a single belief requires solving an infinite-horizon belief-state problem with no closed-form value function. Liu [10] addresses this difficulty by linearizing the unknown decision boundary, leading to a linear system and a closed-form approximate Whittle index. However, the resulting threshold uses only a one-step active--passive comparison and does not account for longer-horizon continuation values.
  We extend this framework to a \emph{$t$-step lookahead threshold policy}. For each subsidy $m$, the threshold is defined by the active-minus-passive advantage under $t$-step finite-horizon value iteration. At $t=1$, the threshold is $m$-independent and recovers the linear threshold of Liu [10]; for $t>1$, it becomes subsidy-dependent through the induced first-crossing structure and tracks the exact decision boundary more closely. The proposed algorithm does not require indexability as an input and includes an indexability verification. Under the original Whittle indexability, we prove that the $t$-step approximate Whittle index converges geometrically to the exact Whittle index, \[ |\widehat W_t(ω)-W(ω)|=O(β^t). \] Numerically, all 2,715 tested three-state instances are verified as indexable according to the proposed criterion. The P95 index error decreases from $2.18\times10^{-2}$ at $t=1$ to $8.93\times10^{-4}$ at $t=8$. In an exact-comparable instance with $β=0.9999$, $t=2$ already recovers the exact Whittle-index ordering. Moderate-depth threshold policies also outperform the one-step baseline and remain close to the optimal dynamic-programming benchmark, while runtime grows mildly with $t$.

</details>


### [178] [PRQ-KMeans: Projection Residual Quantization for Semantic ID Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24207)
*Yunxiao Luo,Siyuan Wang,Ben Chen,Chenyi Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PRQ-KMeans 语义标识符（SID）分词器，从"渐进式共性移除"视角诊断残差量化分词器的三个缺陷，并在事后（post-hoc）设置中通过去除全局均值、Top-k 相似度加权更新质心、以及投影残差替代全码字减除进行修复。在工业级搜索数据集和四个公开推荐基准上取得整体最优性能，HitRate 最高提升 7.4%、MRR 最高提升 11.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义标识符用分层 token 序列表示实体以支持生成式检索与推荐；残差量化分词器逐层选码字并传递残差。作者将这一过程解读为"渐进式共性移除"，由此揭示三个局限：语料级共享分量占用首层容量、硬分配忽略邻近码字的相似度、全码字减除在下一层残差中残留沿选中码字方向的变化。这些局限限制了 SID 的表达质量。

Method: 在事后（post-hoc）设置中提出 PRQ-KMeans：(1) 移除全局均值分量，避免语料级共性占用第一层能力；(2) 采用 Top-k 相似度加权更新来细化质心，缓解硬分配问题；(3) 用投影残差替代全码字减除，即移除表示中沿选中质心方向的分量，从而避免变化残留。该方法不限制于输入重构，专为生成式检索下游任务优化。

Result: 在工业级搜索数据集及四个公开推荐基准上，PRQ-KMeans 在评估的分词器中取得最强综合性能；其中工业数据集上 HitRate 最高提升 7.4%，MRR 最高提升 11.8%。

Conclusion: PRQ-KMeans 通过针对残差量化构建过程的三个缺陷逐一设计修正，显著提升了语义标识符的质量，验证了"渐进式共性移除"视角的有效性，并表明事后设置下的残差构造改进可带来实质性的检索/推荐增益。

Abstract: Semantic identifiers (SIDs) represent entities as hierarchical token sequences for generative retrieval and recommendation. Residual-quantization tokenizers construct these sequences by selecting a codeword at each level and passing a residual to the next. We view this process as progressive commonality removal: each token captures a component shared within its group, while later tokens should model the remaining differences. This view reveals three limitations: a corpus-wide shared component can consume first-level capacity, hard assignment ignores graded similarities to nearby codewords, and full-codeword subtraction can leave variation along the selected-codeword direction in the next residual. We therefore develop our solution in the post-hoc setting, where residual construction is not constrained by input reconstruction. Specifically, we propose PRQ-KMeans, which removes the global-mean component, refines centroids with Top-k similarity-weighted updates, and replaces full-codeword subtraction with a projection residual that removes each representation's selected-centroid component. Experiments on a large-scale industrial search dataset and four public recommendation benchmarks show that PRQ-KMeans achieves the strongest overall performance among the evaluated tokenizers, including gains of up to 7.4% in HitRate and 11.8% in MRR on the industrial dataset.

</details>


### [179] [A Data-dependent Early Stopping Rule using Rademacher Complexity with L1-norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24210)
*Duy Hoang,Bastien Berret,Olivier Bruneau,Laurent Fribourg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练即可估计神经网络最优早停时间的解析框架。该框架基于Rademacher复杂度（RC），并使用L1范数而非L2范数来表示RC，避免了依赖数据分布或协方差矩阵特征值分布的随机矩阵理论假设。该方法针对线性模型与线性回归问题，并借助“线性探测”（linear probing）方法成功推广至非线性神经网络，在MNIST分类任务中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练神经网络需要在拟合训练数据与对未见数据取得稳健性能之间取得平衡，这一泛化能力由训练集经验风险与数据分布期望风险（即泛化误差）之间的差距决定。现有方法通常需要数值估计泛化误差，依赖梯度下降训练和“早停”策略；已有的解析估计虽存在，但大多基于随机矩阵理论，且常对数据分布或协方差矩阵的特征值分布作出假设。因此，作者希望提出一种无需训练、也不依赖上述概率假设的解析框架来估计最优早停时间。

Method: 作者引入了一个基于Rademacher复杂度（RC）的解析框架来估计最优早停时间，无需进行训练。由于理论和数值上的原因，RC采用L1范数而非L2范数表达。方法聚焦于线性模型和线性回归问题，并利用“线性探测”方法将结果推广到非线性神经网络（以MNIST分类为例验证）。

Result: 该框架省去了梯度下降训练和数值化估计的需要，能够解析地给出最优早停时间，且在无需概率分布假设的情况下成立；通过线性探测方法，结果可成功应用于非线性神经网络，并以MNIST分类任务作为示例进行了说明。

Conclusion: 作者提出的解析框架为估计最优早停时间提供了一条不依赖随机矩阵理论假设且无需训练的替代路径，基于L1范数的Rademacher复杂度表达更具理论和数值上的相关性，并能借助线性探测方法扩展到非线性神经网络，从而为实际模型训练中的早停策略选择提供了新的依据。

Abstract: Training neural networks requires balancing the trade-off between fitting the training data and achieving robust performance on unseen inputs. This ability, commonly referred to as generalizability, is determined by the gap between the empirical risk on the training set (``empirical loss'') and the expected risk over the data distribution (``generalization error''). Existing approaches typically estimate the generalization error numerically, requiring gradient descent training and an ``early stopping'' strategy. In this work, we introduce an analytic framework that estimates the optimal time of early stopping without the need for training. Several works in the literature also give such analytical estimations, but they are generally based on random matrix theory and often make assumptions on the distribution of the data or the eigenvalue distribution of the covariance matrix. In contrast, our work is based on Rademacher complexity (RC) without needing such probabilistic assumptions. For both theoretical and numerical reasons, it is more relevant to express RC with the L1- norm rather than with the L2-norm. We focus on the case of linear models and the problem of linear regression. Thanks to the ``linear probing'' method, our results can, however, be successfully applied to nonlinear neural networks, as illustrated in the classification MNIST example.

</details>


### [180] [Contrastive Branch Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24300)
*Ying Wang,Changlin Qiu,Bang Lin,Linbo Jin,Wen Jiang,Zhe Sun,Jingli Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比分支策略优化（CBPO），用于工具集成智能体训练中的细粒度信用分配。CBPO将“分配固定 rollout 预算”与“将分支结果转化为 token 级信用”这两个被现有方法混淆的问题解耦，分别用生成熵筛选分支位置、路径级/节点级衰减分配预算，并通过精确前缀分组内的奖励差异定义对比分支价值（CBV）来重缩放连续动作优势。在十个基准（五个数学推理、五个知识密集检索）和两种模型规模上均取得最高宏平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）使语言模型能够通过与外部工具的多轮交互进行学习，但稀疏的结果奖励无法提供任何信号来判断哪些中间决策导致了成功。分支采样虽然能在备选延续之间产生局部比较，但现有方法往往混淆了两个不同的问题：一是如何分配固定的 rollout 预算，二是如何将分支结果转化为 token 级信用。这种混淆导致探索容易坍缩到少数路径或相邻 token 上，且无法精确地对中间决策进行信用分配。

Method: CBPO 为上述两个问题分别设置专门的机制：(1) 生成熵用于在整个响应中筛选候选分支位置；(2) 路径级和节点级衰减将固定预算分配到不同轨迹和位置，防止探索坍缩。父轨迹与其共享相同 token 前缀的分支构成“精确前缀组”，该受控组内的奖励变化被定义为对比分支价值（CBV）——一种基于结果的局部决策敏感性估计，可在不改变符号的前提下重缩放连续动作的优势。当同一轨迹上选择多个节点时，CBPO 将其划分为不重叠的信用段，从而避免共享 token 上的梯度重复。该方法仅需结果奖励，无需过程级标注。

Result: 在十个基准（包括五个数学推理基准和五个知识密集型检索基准）上的大量实验表明，CBPO 在两个领域及两种模型规模下均持续优于最先进的策略优化方法和基于分支的方法，取得了最高的宏平均准确率。

Conclusion: CBPO 通过解耦预算分配与信用翻译、利用精确前缀局部分组构造无符号翻转的对比价值、并采用非重叠信用分段避免梯度冗余，为工具集成智能体训练提供了一种仅需结果奖励的实用细粒度信用分配方案，并在多个基准上验证了其一致优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) enables language models to learn multi-turn interaction with external tools, yet its sparse outcome rewards provide no signal for identifying which intermediate decisions are responsible for success. Branch sampling induces local comparisons among alternative continuations, but existing methods tend to conflate two distinct problems: allocating a fixed rollout budget and translating branch outcomes into token-level credit. We introduce Contrastive Branch Policy Optimization (CBPO), which disentangles these two problems and assigns a dedicated mechanism to each. Generation entropy screens candidate branch positions across the entire response, while path-level and node-level decay distribute a fixed budget across trajectories and positions to prevent exploration from collapsing onto a few paths or adjacent tokens. A parent trajectory together with the branches that share an identical token prefix forms an exact-prefix group, and the reward variation within this controlled group defines the Contrastive Branch Value (CBV), an outcome-based estimate of local decision sensitivity that rescales continuation advantages without altering their sign. When multiple nodes are selected along the same trajectory, CBPO partitions it into non-overlapping credit segments, thereby avoiding duplicated gradients on shared tokens. Requiring only outcome rewards and no process-level annotation, CBPO provides a practical solution for fine-grained credit assignment in tool-integrated agent training. Extensive experiments on ten benchmarks, including five for mathematical reasoning and five for knowledge-intensive search, show that CBPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art policy-optimization and branch-based methods, attaining the highest macro-average accuracy in both domains and across two model scales.

</details>


### [181] [Causal Analysis for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24303)
*Mathis Jander,Wouter van Heeswijk,Martijn Mes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果分析框架，用于在部署前识别时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的偏差和失效模式。作者对参数化合成时间序列生成器进行干预，并在其他条件不变（ceteris paribus）下测量模型输出的变化，从而检验模型能否保留时间序列模式。将该框架应用于Chronos-2和TimesFM-2.5，针对六种时间序列模式进行测试，发现趋势和谐波振荡模式下的安全配置；同时发现两个模型都倾向于高估持续性，在机制切换模式下出现突然失效，TimesFM-2.5在能量释放模式下失效。这些发现可能源于预训练数据，作者最后给出了模型开发建议、应用选型建议及局限讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从专用时间序列模型转向时间序列基础模型，使模型与应用的关系由一对一变为一对多，引入了集中化风险：许多（可能是高风险的）预测应用会暴露于同一基础模型的相同偏差和失效模式。因此亟需在部署前识别并评估这些模型的偏差与失效模式，以支持安全和可信的落地应用。

Method: 提出基于因果分析（causal analysis）的评估框架：对参数化的合成时间序列生成器进行干预，并在"其他条件不变"（ceteris paribus）条件下测量模型输出的相应变化，以考察时间序列基础模型对时间序列模式的保持能力（pattern preservation）。然后，将该框架应用于Chronos-2和TimesFM-2.5两个模型，在六种不同类型的时间序列模式上进行测试。

Result: 实验发现：两个模型在趋势（trend）与谐波振荡（harmonic oscillation）模式下存在安全配置；两个模型均倾向于高估持续性（overestimating persistence），存在偏差；两个模型在机制切换（regime switch）模式下均突然失效；TimesFM-2.5在能量释放（energy-release）模式下失效。对两个模型原始论文的回顾表明，这些发现可能可由预训练数据解释。

Conclusion: 作者据此提出进一步模型开发的建议、面向应用场景的模型选择（application-specific model selection）建议，并讨论了方法的局限性以及未来的研究方向。

Abstract: Transitioning from bespoke time series models towards time series foundation models changes the relationship of model and application from one-to-one to one-to-many. This shift introduces concentration risk as many, potentially high-risk, forecasting applications are exposed to the same biases and failure modes of a single time series foundation model. At the same time, this centralization allows for economies of scale in model development and validation. In this study we investigate how biases and failure modes of time series foundation models can be identified before deployment. We propose a causal analysis framework to investigate the ability of a time series foundation model to preserve time series patterns. To achieve this, we intervene on parameterized synthetic time series generators and measure the corresponding change in model output under ceteris paribus conditions. We apply our causal analysis framework to Chronos-2 and TimesFM-2.5 and test them across six distinct time series patterns. We find safe configurations for trend and harmonic oscillation patterns. The results also indicate a bias in both models towards overestimating persistence, sudden failures for both models against the regime switch pattern and failure for TimesFM-2.5 against the energy-release pattern. Our review of the original works for both models indicates that the findings might be explained by the data used for pretraining. We conclude our study with suggestions for further model development, recommendations for application-specific model selection, and a discussion of limitations and further research directions.

</details>


### [182] [A Structural FHMM for Interpretable Disease Trajectories in T2DM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24328)
*Alessandro Mari,Ekaterina Krymova,Guillaume Obozinski,Maria Luisa Marques de Sa Faquetti,Adrian Martinez de la Torre,Andrea Burden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因子隐含马尔可夫模型（FHMM）的结构变体，用于分析2型糖尿病（T2DM）患者的疾病轨迹。模型将患者的潜在健康状态表示为多个独立、同时演化的组分（对应合并症与实验室结果），实现了对临床有意义患者状态的识别和常见疾病轨迹的聚类。基于2006至2019年首次开具非胰岛素抗糖尿病药处方的THIN电子健康记录数据，模型识别出与已知糖尿病并发症模式对应的多个临床一致潜伏组分，并揭示了异质性进展通路（包括微血管主导型与多器官型轨迹），后者与更高合并症负担和死亡率相关。结果表明该框架能捕捉EHR中有意义的纵向结构，提供对T2DM及其合并症演进的可解释洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以刻画T2DM患者多病共存且随时间演变的复杂健康状态。作者希望利用结构化潜变量模型，在无监督条件下从大规模电子健康记录中识别临床有意义的患者状态和典型疾病进展轨迹，为理解糖尿病并发症的异质性提供可解释的工具。

Method: 提出因子隐含马尔可夫模型（FHMM）的结构变体：患者潜在健康状态由多个相互独立且同时演化的组分构成，每个组分与特定合并症和实验室检验结果相关联。该结构化潜表示使状态识别与轨迹聚类具有可解释性。数据来源于IQVIA医学研究数据（含THIN数据库），纳入2006年1月至2019年12月首次开具非胰岛素抗糖尿病药物处方的T2DM患者。

Result: 模型识别出多个与已知糖尿病并发症模式相符的临床一致潜在组分，并发现异质性进展通路：包括微血管主导型和多器官型轨迹，后者与更高的合并症负担和死亡率相关。实验结果表明该框架能够捕捉EHR中有意义的纵向结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的FHMM结构变体为T2DM及其合并症的长期演进提供了可解释的纵向建模视角，能够识别临床相关的患者亚型和轨迹，有望辅助理解疾病异质性并支持临床决策。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a structural variant of the Factorial Hidden Markov Model (FHMM) for the analysis of disease trajectories in patients with Type 2 diabetes mellitus (T2DM). The model represents a patient's latent health state as a combination of multiple independent, simultaneously evolving components, associated with comorbidities and lab results. This structured latent representation facilitates the identification of clinically meaningful patient states and clustering of common disease trajectories. We evaluate the proposed approach using The IQVIA Medical Research Data incorporating data from THIN, a Cegedim database of anonymized electronic health records (EHR), identifying patients with a first-ever prescription for a non-insulin antidiabetic drug (NIAD) between January 2006 and December 2019. The model identifies multiple clinically coherent latent components corresponding to known patterns of diabetes-related complications and reveals heterogeneous progression pathways, including distinct microvascular-dominant and multi-organ trajectories associated with elevated comorbidity burden and mortality. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework captures meaningful longitudinal structure in EHR data and provides interpretable insights into the evolution of T2DM and its comorbidities.

</details>


### [183] [When Does Self-Supervised Pretraining Help Tabular Models? A Study of Label Scarcity and Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24381)
*Sahand Mazrouei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了"掩码-重建"自监督预训练目标在表格数据上的有效性，尤其是在极端标签稀缺（仅5%-10%标签）和测试时缺失场景下。结果表明：SSL平均优于从头训练并与随机森林等树集成方法相当，但在统计上不显著且任务间方差大；SSL在干净数据集上提升最可靠，而在天然高缺失率数据集上反而常造成性能下降；在测试时缺失（MCAR与MNAR）情境下SSL平均AUC更高，但经多重比较校正后仍不显著；与VIME、SCARF、SubTab三种自监督基线相比无显著差异，说明结论具有一般性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在表格数据上虽被广泛看好，但其在极端标签稀缺与测试时缺失情境下的实际效能缺乏严格的实证检验。作者旨在通过系统性、统计严谨的对照实验，澄清"SSL是否真的优于从头训练"以及"缺失值重建目标是否必然有益于天然带缺失的数据集"这两项被广泛默认却未被充分验证的假设。

Method: 在14个多样化的分类任务上，将掩码-重建型SSL预训练目标与从头训练及经典基线（随机森林等树集成）进行对比；并在不同标签比例（5%、10%）下评估。此外，分别注入完全随机缺失（MCAR）和结构化缺失（MNAR）以考察测试时缺失鲁棒性；最后在相同编码器架构下与VIME、SCARF、SubTab三种既有表格SSL基线对比，并采用Holm-Bonferroni校正进行多重比较。

Result: (1) SSL平均优于从头训练（10%标签时AUC约0.8954 vs. 随机森林0.9015），但提升不显著（p=0.626）且任务间方差大；(2) SSL在有天然缺失的数据集上常导致性能退化，反而在干净数据集上提升最可靠；(3) 测试时缺失下SSL平均AUC更高（MCAR +0.0245，11/14任务为正；MNAR +0.0418，8/14任务为正），但校正后均不显著（adjusted p=0.118与0.518）；(4) 与VIME、SCARF、SubTab比较均无显著差异（adjusted p=0.459, 1.000, 1.000）。

Conclusion: 表格数据SSL的平均增益真实存在但统计上不稳定、因任务而异，且高度依赖数据特征（在干净数据上更可靠，在天然高缺失数据上反而有害）。作者推断该发现反映的是表格SSL的一般性质而非某个特定预训练任务的特性，呼吁对该领域普遍宣称的收益持审慎态度，并提示需要更多条件化（"何时有效"）的研究。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising approach for tabular data, yet its efficacy under extreme label scarcity and test-time missingness remains under-explored. In this paper, we evaluate a mask-and-recover SSL pretraining objective against training from scratch and classical baselines across 14 diverse classification tasks. First, while SSL outperforms training from scratch on average and remains competitive with state-of-the-art tree ensembles (achieving ~0.8954 AUC vs. Random Forest's 0.9015 at 10% labels), the SSL-vs-scratch gains exhibit high inter-task variance and lack significance (p = 0.626 at both 5% and 10% labels). Second, contrary to the hypothesis that missing-value imputation objectives universally benefit datasets with native missingness, SSL yields the most reliable improvements on clean datasets, while frequently degrading performance on datasets with high inherent missingness. Third, despite this training variance, SSL-pretrained models achieve a higher average AUC than scratch-trained models under both test-time missingness completely at random (MCAR) injection (+0.0245 AUC, positive on 11 of 14 tasks) and structured missingness shifts (MNAR, +0.0418 AUC, positive on 8 of 14 tasks), though neither difference remains statistically significant after Holm-Bonferroni correction for multiple comparisons (adjusted p = 0.118 and p = 0.518, respectively). Fourth, comparing our mask-and-recover objective against three established tabular SSL baselines (VIME, SCARF, SubTab) under an identical encoder architecture, we find no significant difference from any of them (adjusted p = 0.459, p = 1.000, p = 1.000), indicating our findings reflect general properties of tabular SSL rather than idiosyncrasies of one particular pretext task.

</details>


### [184] [Equivariant Covariance Tensors: Guaranteed SPD Uncertainty for Tensor-Valued Geometric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24386)
*Ruihan Liu,Yu Ji,Jianbo Yu,Shifu Yan,Qingchao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对对称秩-2张量预测的 E(3)-等变不确定性量化（UQ）框架，同时建模均值与协方差以保持旋转对称性。协方差经不可约表示分解（2×l=0 ⊕ 2×l=2 ⊕ 1×l=4），并借助矩阵指数映射从平直李代数 𝔰𝔶𝔪(6) 映射到 SPD 流形以保证正定性；训练采用基于多元拉普拉斯分布的 Log-Euclidean 等变评分目标（LE-ESO），在 ModelNet40 惯量张量与 Materials Project 介电张量上验证了竞争性精度与物理一致的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)-等变神经网络虽在张量值点估计上表现优异，但缺乏严格的不确定性度量。对对称秩-2张量（六维 Kelvin–Mandel 坐标）的完整不确定性需用 6×6 协方差矩阵表征，而如何在保持旋转对称性的同时对其做合理的概率建模与量化仍是开放难题。作者旨在为这类几何深度学习输出提供严格、物理一致且具备风险/OOD敏感性的 UQ 方法。

Method: 构建 E(3)-等变预测分布：均值与协方差均满足旋转对称性。协方差按 Sym²(ρ_c) ≅ 2×(l=0) ⊕ 2×(l=2) ⊕ 1×(l=4) 分解为不可约表示，借助矩阵指数将平直李代数 𝔰𝔶𝔪(6) 中的参数映射到弯曲 SPD 流形，严格保证正定性并保持精确等变性。训练损失采用 Log-Euclidean 等变评分目标（LE-ESO），即基于多元拉普拉斯分布的鲁棒替代损失，既对重尾误差稳健又提供稳定优化。

Result: 在 ModelNet40 惯量张量与 Materials Project 介电张量数据集上的验证表明，该方法取得竞争性预测性能，并提供物理一致、保持对称性的不确定性估计，且具备有效的风险度量与 OOD 敏感性。

Conclusion: 该框架为等变张量预测提供了首类严格的协方差级不确定性量化方案，在保持等变性的同时解决了 SPD 正定约束与鲁棒训练问题，有望推广到其他张量值几何深度学习任务。

Abstract: Tensor-valued prediction is fundamental to geometric deep learning, yet uncertainty quantification (UQ) for such outputs remains an open challenge. While E(3)-equivariant neural networks excel at point estimates, they lack rigorous confidence measures. We focus on symmetric rank-2 tensor prediction, where the target has six Kelvin--Mandel coordinates and full uncertainty is represented by a $6\times6$ covariance matrix. We introduce a framework for E(3)-equivariant UQ, modeling the full predictive distribution where both mean and covariance preserve rotational symmetry. Our approach decomposes the covariance into irreducible representations $\mathrm{Sym}^2(ρ_c) \cong 2\times(l=0) \oplus 2\times(l=2) \oplus 1\times(l=4)$. By mapping from the flat Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sym}(6)$ to the curved SPD manifold via matrix exponentiation, we strictly ensure positive-definite covariances while maintaining exact equivariance. Furthermore, we formulate a Log-Euclidean Equivariant Scoring Objective (LE-ESO)---a robust surrogate loss based on the Multivariate Laplace distribution---providing robustness to heavy-tailed errors and stable optimization. Validation on ModelNet40 inertia tensors and Materials Project dielectric tensors demonstrates that our method achieves competitive performance and provides physically consistent, symmetry-preserving uncertainty estimates with useful risk and OOD sensitivity.

</details>


### [185] [Joint Distribution Alignment for Universal Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24429)
*Shizhe Li,Hongshan Pu,Mengying Xie,Yi Xiang,Xiaowei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统无监督域适应（UDA）假设源域与目标域标签空间完全一致的局限性，本文研究了标签空间可能不同的通用域适应（UniDA）问题，首次给出UniDA泛化误差上界，并据此提出JAUA算法，通过Chi-Square散度对齐联合分布、配合渐进式伪标签方法，在六个公开图像数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UDA仅处理样本分布漂移问题，但在真实应用中源域与目标域的标签空间往往存在差异（既包含样本分布漂移，又包含类别空间差异），即通用域适应（UniDA）场景。现有UniDA工作缺乏理论分析，本文旨在填补这一理论空白并据此设计算法。

Method: 1) 理论上：推导了通用域适应的泛化误差上界；2) 算法上：基于该误差界提出JAUA（Joint Distribution Alignment for Universal Domain Adaptation），通过最小化以Chi-Square散度计算的分布差异来对齐源域与目标域的联合分布；3) 训练策略上：提出渐进式伪标签方法，为目标域无标签样本赋予伪标签以增强对齐效果。

Result: 在六个公开图像数据集上的实验表明，JAUA在解决UniDA问题上优于现有方法，验证了所提理论界与算法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 本文为通用域适应提供了首个泛化误差理论分析，并基于该理论构建了有效的JAUA算法，证明联合分布对齐与渐进式伪标签相结合能够有效处理源、目标域标签空间不一致的UniDA场景。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) has been widely concerned in the fields of machine learning, pattern recognition, and computer vision. Traditional UDA learning usually assumes that the label spaces of the source and target domains are exactly the same and only needs to solve the problem of sample distribution drift existing between two domains. However, in real world applications, the label spaces between two domains may be different. In this case, there are both sample distribution drift and class spatial difference between domains, namely Universal Domain Adaptation (UniDA) learning scenario. At present, existing works rarely offer theoretical analysis for universal domain adaptation. In this paper, we provide an upper bound of the generalization error for universal domain adaptation. According to the proposed generalization error bound, we propose a novel UniDA algorithm called Joint Distribution Alignment for Universal Domain Adaptation (JAUA), which aligns the joint distributions by minimizing the distribution discrepancy calculated by Chi-Square divergence. Furthermore, we propose a progressive pseudo-labeling method to assign the pseudo labels to unlabeled target samples. The experiment results on six public image datasets demonstrate the superiority of JAUA in handling the UniDA problem.

</details>


### [186] [Evaluating Deep Multivariate Imputation Models on Wearable Device Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24436)
*Skye Goodman,Roussel Desmond Nzoyem,Leandro Junges,Peter Kissack,Yasser Qureshi,Amberly Brigden,Jeff Clark,Nawid Keshtmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对可穿戴设备数据的现实缺失机制（同一物理传感器对应的特征成组缺失，即结构化缺失），本文提出了一种可迁移的评估协议：从训练集挖掘连续的缺失段模板，按各特征缺口长度分位数分层后以保留共缺失结构的块掩码注入测试数据；同时提出与之匹配的训练协议。在单参与者（Garmin手表记录的癫痫患者）数据上，该训练协议使BRITS的严重缺口MAE降低43%。此外作者扩展了BRITS（加入时刻编码与昼夜谐波通道）。结论是没有单一模型全面占优：线性插值在短缺口慢变特征上最优；扩展BRITS在中/重度缺口的动态心电特征上MAE更低；SAITS虽MAE更高但以Jensen-Shannon距离衡量能更好保持真实分布。模型排名高度依赖评估设计，传统随机点式评估掩盖了真实能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度缺失填补方法（如BRITS、SAITS）在真实多模态生理数据、真实缺失性场景下评估不足；既有基准采用随机点式留出协议，错误地假设缺失在特征间和时间上相互独立，而可穿戴数据实际上因传感器共享而存在结构化缺失（特征成块共同丢失），导致模型能力被误判。

Method: 1) 提出评估协议：从训练数据中挖掘连续的缺失段模板，按每个特征的缺口长度分位数对其进行分层，再以保留共缺失结构的块掩码方式注入测试数据，从而还原真实的缺失相关性。2) 提出匹配的训练协议：让模型暴露于相同缺失分布。3) 扩展BRITS：引入时刻（time-of-day）编码与昼夜谐波通道。4) 在单参与者Garmin智能手表记录的数据上，对线性插值、BRITS、扩展BRITS、SAITS等进行多维度比较（MAE与Jensen-Shannon距离）。

Result: 匹配训练协议将BRITS在严重缺口上的MAE降低43%；扩展BRITS在动态心电特征的中度和严重缺口上取得更低MAE；线性插值在短缺口、慢变特征上最优；SAITS在更高MAE的前提下以Jensen-Shannon距离更优地保持真实值分布。没有单一模型在所有情境下占优，且模型排名强烈依赖评估设计。

Conclusion: 传统随机点式评估协议因忽略共缺失结构而掩盖了真实模型能力；本文提出的可迁移评估与训练协议更能暴露模型优劣，是迈向未来多传感器可穿戴数据集上更优缺失填补策略的关键步骤。同时也说明应根据特征动态特性与缺口严重程度选择合适方法（插值、深度模型或分布保持型模型）。

Abstract: Wearable device data enables continuous health monitoring, but suffers from structured missingness: features sharing a physical sensor drop out together. Deep imputation methods such as BRITS and SAITS have seen limited evaluation on multimodal physiological data under realistic missingness, and existing benchmarks use random-point holdout protocols that incorrectly assume missingness is independent across features and time. Using data from a person with epilepsy recorded on a Garmin smartwatch, we develop an evaluation protocol that mines contiguous missing-run templates from training data, stratifies them by per-feature gap-length quantiles, and injects them as block masks with preserved co-missingness structure. A matched training protocol exposing models to the same missingness distribution reduces BRITS's severe-gap MAE by 43%, demonstrating the potential benefit of the proposed evaluation and training protocol within this single-participant dataset. We further extend BRITS with time-of-day encoding and a circadian harmonic channel. No single model dominates: linear interpolation is optimal for slow-moving features over short gaps; extended BRITS achieves lower MAE on dynamic cardiac features in moderate and severe gaps; and SAITS better preserves the ground-truth distribution by Jensen-Shannon distance despite higher MAE. Ultimately, model rankings strongly depend on evaluation designs. By exposing how traditional evaluation methods obscure true model capabilities, our transferable protocol establishes critical steps towards developing better imputation strategies for future multi-sensor wearable datasets.

</details>


### [187] [WarpSAC: Towards the Pinnacle of Scalable Off-policy RL by Rethinking Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24479)
*Zihao Wu,Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Huizhong Song,Pengyi Li,Yifu Yuan,Fei Ni,Jinyi Liu,Wei Wei,Jianrong Wang,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模并行仿真下离策略强化学习（off-policy RL）的稳定器（stabilizers，如参数归一化、截断双Q、重放加权等）在不同数据规模下的适用性，发现其具有数据体制依赖性。据此提出WarpSAC系列算法：通过Sample Weight Decay提升开发效率，提供面向数据受限CPU训练的WarpSAC-L与面向数据充裕GPU并行训练的WarpSAC-A两种变体。相比FlashSAC，在9个CPU环境和14个GPU并行环境上分别提升归一化分数-步数AUC 4.5%和23.1%，将UnitreeG1TransportBox-v1成功率从19.8%提升至96.4%，并实现更快的sim-to-real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行仿真使离线策略强化学习的数据规模发生根本性变化，而现有稳定器大多是为数据受限的重放缓冲（classical replay）设计的。这些稳定器在不同数据体制下的有效性缺乏系统认知，导致算法无法适应数据充裕的新训练场景，亟需厘清数据体制与稳定器之间的依赖关系并据此重新设计可扩展的离策略算法。

Method: (1) 通过8个基准族上的受控实验系统比较参数归一化（parameter normalization）、截断双Q（clipped double-Q）与基于年龄的重放加权（age-biased replay weighting）等稳定器在不同重放覆盖范围和数据丰富度下的表现；(2) 基于实验发现提出WarpSAC家族：采用Sample Weight Decay机制实现高效开发，并针对两种数据体制提供变体——WarpSAC-L（启用参数归一化、使用截断双Q，面向数据受限的CPU规模训练）与WarpSAC-A（关闭归一化、使用单Q，面向数据充裕的GPU并行训练）。

Result: WarpSAC在9个CPU规模环境上相比FlashSAC将归一化分数-步数AUC提升4.5%，在14个GPU并行环境上提升23.1%；将UnitreeG1TransportBox-v1成功率由19.8%提高到96.4%；在MuJoCo Playground上平均归一化墙钟时间AUC提升19.1%；在Unitree G1上实现比FlashSAC快36.4%的sim-to-real部署。

Conclusion: 可扩展的离策略强化学习应依据可用数据体制来调整其稳定器：数据充裕时过度依赖保守稳定器会限制价值拟合与学习效率，而数据受限时仍需保守设计。WarpSAC作为数据体制感知的算法族验证了这一原则，表明面向大规模并行仿真的RL需要将数据规模纳入算法设计考量。

Abstract: Massively parallel simulation changes the data regime in which off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is trained, challenging stabilizers designed for data-limited replay. Through controlled experiments across eight benchmark families, we show that these stabilizers are data-regime-dependent: parameter normalization helps with narrow replay coverage but restricts value fitting when data are abundant, while clipped double-Q can be relaxed in high-throughput manipulation. Age-biased replay weighting improves learning efficiency across regimes, especially with limited network capacity.
  Based on these findings, we propose WarpSAC, a regime-aware family of off-policy RL algorithms. WarpSAC uses Sample Weight Decay for efficient exploitation and provides two variants: WarpSAC-L (Norm ON, clipped double-Q) for data-limited CPU-scale training, and WarpSAC-A (Norm OFF, single-Q) for data-abundant GPU-parallel training. WarpSAC improves normalized score--step AUC over FlashSAC by 4.5% across nine CPU-scale environments and 23.1% across fourteen GPU-parallel environments. It increases UnitreeG1TransportBox-v1 success rate from 19.8% to 96.4%, improves mean normalized wall-time AUC on MuJoCo Playground by 19.1%, and achieves 36.4% faster sim-to-real deployment on Unitree G1 than FlashSAC. These results show that scalable off-policy RL should adapt its stabilizers to the available data regime.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Static Interpretability: Anticipating Post-SFT Mechanisms from Pre-SFT Parameters for Better Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24482)
*Hang Chen,Jiaying Zhu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"前向定位"（forward-looking localization）框架，利用仅有的预SFT参数和目标数据集来预测微调后的可解释性状态，从而克服传统机械可解释性只能事后回溯、无法指导新任务SFT的局限。该方法以泰勒展开理论建模SFT为连续参数演化过程，并设计神经元级与组件级双粒度定位流程，实验表明其能提供更优的SFT引导，且随模型规模扩大表现出稳健的性能与时间可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"定位-再微调"（locating-then-tuning）范式依赖机械可解释性来识别关键参数，但机械可解释性是回溯性的：直接解释预SFT模型会引入误导性结论，尤其是对全新任务，初始识别的神经元与最终支配模型的神经元截然不同，这种偏差会主动干扰SFT过程。

Method: 作者提出前向定位框架：1）理论上将SFT建模为连续参数演化，借助泰勒展开严格地桥接"微调后的机械目标"与"预SFT模型的动态梯度"；2）实践上设计双粒度（神经元级与组件级）定位流水线，仅需预SFT参数与目标数据集即可准确估计post-SFT的可解释性状态。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架不仅能提供更优的SFT引导，而且在模型规模不断增大时表现出稳健的性能与时间可扩展性（temporal scalability）。

Conclusion: 该工作超越了传统可解释性的根本局限——即无法在任务机制被训练之前识别其关键机制，开创了将机械可解释性与定向优化相统一的前瞻性（预测性）研究前沿。

Abstract: Mechanistic Localization bridges mechanistic interpretability and post-training optimization by isolating critical parameters via interpretative approaches and then guiding parameter-efficient Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) in a ``locating-then-tuning'' paradigm. However, due to the retrospective nature of mechanistic interpretability, directly interpreting pre-SFT models introduces misleading conclusions. Specifically for novel tasks, initially identified neurons differ drastically from those governing the final model, introducing biases that actively disrupt SFT. To address this, we propose a forward-looking localization framework that accurately estimates the post-SFT interpretability state using only pre-SFT parameters and the target dataset. Theoretically, we model SFT as a continuous parameter evolution, leveraging Taylor expansion to rigorously bridge the post-tuning mechanistic objective with the pre-SFT model's dynamic gradients. Practically, we design dual-granularity (neuron- and component-level) localization pipelines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach not only provides superior SFT guidance but also exhibits robust performance and temporal scalability across increasing model sizes. This work transcends the fundamental limitation of traditional interpretability-its inability to identify task-critical mechanisms before they are trained-pioneering a predictive frontier that unites mechanistic interpretability with targeted optimization.

</details>


### [189] [Where Entropy Is Measured Matters: Policy Geometry in Bounded Continuous-Control PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24488)
*Yiyang He,Zhichun Zhou,Ziwei Wang,Tao Xue,Haolin Fei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究PPO在连续控制中「有界动作」策略的几何形态，发现熵在何处测量（潜在高斯熵H(u) vs 实际动作熵H(a)）会显著改变策略均值与方差的耦合方式。在80肌肉MyoLeg任务中，潜在熵导致策略均值大量落在可执行区间之外且方差偏大；而实际动作熵通过变换雅可比为均值提供向内的梯度，使动作更靠近区间内部。对照实验（无熵项、直接均值惩罚、Dog-Stand复现）表明，均值分布在两种熵测量下可复现，但相同均值几何下方差与回报仍可大不相同。结论是熵测量空间是均值-方差耦合的设计选择，仅凭任务回报无法刻画有界策略的几何特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多连续控制策略以无界高斯分布优化后再映射到有界动作空间，但现有研究中熵正则项通常施加在潜在高斯上，其与有界动作映射的相互作用未被充分理解。作者观察到裁剪后的高斯有大量动作落在边界附近（89.07%的动作在边界5%以内），且即使方差为零仍有83.83%的动作接近边界，说明问题不仅源于方差，而是策略均值本身偏移。因此需要系统分析熵测量位置对策略几何的系统性影响。

Method: 在PPO框架下对比三种设置：潜在高斯熵H(u)、无熵项、实际动作熵H(a)。推导熵损失对均值与方差的梯度：H(u)对均值梯度为零、对方差提供恒定的增方差梯度；H(a)则通过变换雅可比额外引入均值的内向梯度。实验采用80肌肉MyoLeg任务与38维Dog-Stand复现（独立于CleanRL的PPO实现），并辅以同状态评估、边界余量1%-10%扫描和直接均值惩罚等对照分析。

Result: 在MyoLeg三个匹配种子下，近边界占用率分别为：潜在熵71.42%、无熵29.76%、实际动作熵18.83%。tanh映射不能消除高方差区间。Dog-Stand复现再现了均值几何的排序，且该排序在共享状态评估和1%-10%边界余量下均成立。直接均值惩罚可匹配甚至超过H(a)的居中效果，说明内部均值并非实际动作熵独有；但匹配的均值几何可与显著不同的方差和回报共存。

Conclusion: 熵测量空间（潜在 vs 实际动作）是均值-方差耦合的设计选择，不同的测量位置会系统地改变有界策略的几何。仅仅优化任务回报不足以表征有界策略的分布几何，需要将熵测量位置纳入策略设计的显式考量。
